Result: ResNet的跳跃连接与Transformer的注意力机制在改变链接数的能力上等价；ResNet和Transformer严格强于使用单调激活的前馈网络；单调激活前馈网络强于可逆流模型；非单调激活可使前馈网络达到与ResNet和Transformer同等的表达力类别。

Conclusion: 低维拓扑（尤其是链接数分析）可作为评估和指导AI架构设计的有用工具。不同架构在拓扑不变量改变能力上的层次关系得到了严格刻画。

Abstract: We study layered models, including feedforward networks, ResNets, and transformers, by limiting each layer to a width of $d = 3$, i.e., $\mathbb{R}^3$ as representation space. This allows us to track how a neural network changes low-dimensional topological invariants through its layers. Just about any topological structure may be simplified or even trivialized by simply increasing dimension; e.g., any knot is equivalent to an unknot in $\mathbb{R}^4$. By restricting to $\mathbb{R}^3$, we not only isolate the effects of activation and depth from that of width, we work in a space that lends itself to easy visualization. We focus on linking number here, deferring other invariants like link groups, Milnor's $\barμ$-invariants, knot types, ambient cobordisms, to a sequel. We provide full proofs and empirical experiments to justify the following insights: When measured by their power to effect changes in linking numbers, the layer-skipping feature in ResNets is as powerful as the attention mechanism in transformers; both ResNets and transformers are strictly more powerful than feedforward neural networks with monotonic activations, which are in turn more powerful than invertible and flow-based models; but replacing monotonic activation with a nonmonotonic one elevates a feedforward network into the same expressivity class as ResNets and transformers. These results suggest that low-dimensional topology can be a useful tool to guide designs of AI architectures. We also generalize our results from $d = 3$ to arbitrary $d > 3$.

</details>


### [75] [Review Residuals: Update-Conditioned Residual Gating for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31859)
*Kyle Kramer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Review Residuals，通过可学习的输入相关门控对残差连接中的更新进行缩放，在大规模（590M-1B参数）时显著优于标准残差和Highway门控，且深度稳定性更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准残差连接以固定系数1直接添加每个子层的更新，网络在提交更新前从不评估其可靠性。受人为因素中独立验证原则启发，引入Review Residuals机制。

Method: 核心公式：h_l = h_{l-1} + r_l * u_l，其中门控r_l = sigmoid(W[RMSNorm(h_{l-1}), RMSNorm(u_l)])。门控同时条件于当前状态和提议更新，区别于先前门控残差方法。比较了凸形式（Highway风格）和加性恒等保持形式。

Result: 两个发现：(1) 深度稳定性：Highway风格的凸门控在约20层以上重新引入梯度消失无法训练，而加性恒等保持形式在所有测试深度稳定训练。(2) 随规模涌现：在60M-1B五个规模上训练，小规模无优势，但在590M时显著优于参数匹配的Highway门控和标准残差（p<0.05），1B时优势更大。优势随模型规模增长而非缩小。

Conclusion: Review Residuals在大规模模型中具有显著优势，其加性恒等保持的门控形式具有良好的深度稳定性，且优势随规模扩大而增强。

Abstract: Residual connections add every sublayer's proposed update with a fixed coefficient of one; the network never evaluates whether an update is reliable before committing it. Drawing on the human-factors principle of independent verification, we introduce Review Residuals, which scale each update by a learned, input-dependent gate conditioned on both the current state and the proposed update: h_l = h_{l-1} + r_l * u_l with r_l = sigmoid(W[RMSNorm(h_{l-1}), RMSNorm(u_l)]). Conditioning the gate on the update is the property that distinguishes it from prior gated and scaled residuals. We report two findings. First, a depth-stability result: a convex (Highway-style) form of the gate reintroduces vanishing gradients and fails to train beyond ~20 layers, whereas the additive, identity-preserving form trains stably at all depths we tested. Second, an emergence-with-scale result: trained from scratch across five sizes (60M-1B parameters, multi-seed), Review Residuals show no advantage at small scale but at 590M significantly outperform both a parameter-matched Highway gate and a parameter-matched standard residual (p<0.05), with a larger advantage at 1B. The benefit grows with model size rather than shrinking.

</details>


### [76] [Sequential RC-TGAN: Generating Relational Time Series with Spectral Envelope Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31904)
*Mohamed Gueye,Yazid Attabi,Manuel Morales,Maxime Dumas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Seq. RC-TGAN，一种基于谱包络理论的可微分损失函数来生成保留周期结构的合成关系数据库时间序列，并引入谱密度散度与谱包络散度两种新评估指标，在真实与模拟数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成关系数据库中的时间序列（如分类状态码）存在显著的周期性与季节性结构，但标准编码方法（如one-hot）无法捕获频域特征，且缺乏评估频域保真度的标准化指标。

Method: 在RC-TGAN基础上引入基于谱包络理论的可微损失函数，使生成器能够通过反向传播直接优化潜在周期结构；对连续时间序列采用变分高斯混合模型（VGM）离散化策略以适用谱包络理论；设计由参数α控制的模拟分类时间序列生成器，其理论谱包络已知，从而建立严格基准评估框架。

Result: 在真实数据集与模拟基准上的实验表明，该方法在复现循环模式与长期季节性方面显著优于现有最先进系统，涵盖分类与连续特征。

Conclusion: 通过频域正则化损失与新的评估指标，Seq. RC-TGAN有效提升了关系数据库时间序列生成的频域保真度，填补了该领域评估标准缺失的空白。

Abstract: The generation of synthetic relational databases often involves modeling complex temporal dynamics, such as transaction logs or event sequences. A significant challenge in this domain is the handling of categorical time series (e.g., status codes), where standard encoding methods like one-hot encoding fail to capture intrinsic frequency-domain features such as seasonality and cyclicity. In this paper, we introduce Sequential RC-TGAN (Seq. RC-TGAN), a temporal extension of the RC-TGAN framework, equipped with a novel integrated loss function based on the \textit{Spectral Envelope Theory}. This differentiable loss allows the generator to directly optimize the preservation of latent periodic structures via backpropagation. While spectral envelope theory is inherently designed for categorical sequences, we extend this frequency-domain regularization to continuous time series by employing a Variational Gaussian Mixture Model (VGM) discretization strategy. To establish a mathematically rigorous evaluation standard, we simulate categorical time series governed by a parameter $α$, with exactly known theoretical spectral envelopes. Integrating these dynamic sequences into the child tables of a relational database yields a robust ground-truth benchmark for evaluating the frequency-domain fidelity of our generative framework. Furthermore, we address the lack of robust evaluation standards for relational time series by proposing two new metrics: Spectral Density Divergence and Spectral Envelope Divergence. Experimental results on real-world datasets, as well as our simulated benchmarks, demonstrate that our end-to-end approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art systems in reproducing cyclic patterns and long-term seasonality across both categorical and continuous features.

</details>


### [77] [Making Sense of Touch from the Child's View for Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31943)
*Max Whitton,Zecheng Wang,Puchen Liu,Quang Tuan Truong,Shengao Wang,Manaswi Yadamreddy,Oktay Ozel,Visista Jayanti,Saniya Sekhon,Hanna Samuel Tadesse,Lawrence Miao,Junjie Wang,Jiasen Lu,Chen Yu,Boqing Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨触觉在婴儿视觉概念学习中的作用，通过构建结构化编码系统收集26.4万段触觉事件视频片段，并利用该数据集预训练发展性模型，量化分析触觉对婴儿视觉学习的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究触觉是否是婴儿学习视觉概念的机制，并量化其重要性，以理解婴儿在学习过程中对触觉的依赖程度。

Method: 提出婴儿中心触觉事件的结构化编码系统，构建包含26.4万段两秒触觉事件视频片段的数据集，并基于此数据集预训练发展性模型。

Result: 成功构建了大规模触觉事件数据集，预训练的发展性模型揭示了婴儿从触觉中学习的有前景的见解。

Conclusion: 触觉在婴儿视觉概念学习中具有重要作用，通过结构化编码和模型预训练可以量化其贡献程度。

Abstract: Is the sense of touch a mechanism for human babies' learning of visual concepts? If so, can we quantify its importance, and to what extent do babies rely on their sense of touch for visual learning? To approach these questions in a principled way, we propose a structured coding system for baby-centric touch events, yielding a dataset of 264k two-second clips of touch events coded according to this system. Using this dataset, we pretrain developmentally grounded models that reveal promising insights into the nature of baby learning from touch.

</details>


### [78] [Signed-Permutation Coordinate Transport for RMSNorm Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31963)
*John Sweeney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM中残差流存在架构依赖的规范自由度：LayerNorm为置换规范(S_d)，RMSNorm为带符号置换规范(B_d)。符号边缘化的匈牙利匹配消除了符号混淆导致的排列精度上限。基于规范保持的坐标传输在跨运行坐标恢复（91.1% vs 60.3%）、SAE重建、情感/拒绝控制、合并行为及AdamW状态恢复上均显著优于纯排列对齐。最终指出索引级可解释性声明仅在显式规范下可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM工作流中，跨检查点操作（如迁移导向向量、稀疏自编码器、Top-K神经元集、归因列表及合并对齐）依赖残差流的规范固定，但不同架构（LayerNorm vs RMSNorm）的规范自由度不同，现有排列对齐方法对RMSNorm模型存在对称性不完整问题。

Method: 提出符号边缘化的匈牙利匹配（sign-marginalized Hungarian matching）解决符号混淆；将规范保持的坐标传输（coordinate-preserving transport）作为主要操作对象，沿同基微调轨迹组合局部B_d规范恢复跨运行坐标。

Result: 跨运行坐标恢复：91.1%（1500步）vs 60.3%（端点匹配）；TinyLlama SAE重建NMSE：0.004（B_d）vs 1.08（S_d）；Qwen情感控制保持95.8%效果 vs 17.2%；拒绝控制在S_d下符号反转；AdamW状态仅在符号保持传输下恢复轨迹。

Conclusion: LLM残差流具有架构依赖的规范自由度，符号边缘化对齐是RMSNorm模型的必要步骤，索引级可解释性声明需在显式规范下才有意义。

Abstract: Modern LLM workflows move coordinate-indexed objects across checkpoints: steering vectors, sparse autoencoders, top-$k$ neuron sets, attribution lists, and merge alignments. This is only well posed after fixing the model's residual-stream gauge, which we show is architecture-dependent: LayerNorm residual charts have permutation gauge $S_d$ (up to a global sign flip), while RMSNorm charts with generic per-channel gain have signed-permutation gauge $B_d = S_d \ltimes \{\pm 1\}^d$. Permutation-only alignment is therefore symmetry-incomplete for RMSNorm models. We introduce sign-marginalized Hungarian matching and prove a sharp failure mode: with decorrelated coordinates, raw signed-correlation matching has a structural permutation-accuracy ceiling at the positive-sign fraction of the true gauge, which sign-marginalization removes. We then make coordinate-preserving transport, not function-level merging, the primary object: composing saved-checkpoint local $B_d$ gauges along same-base fine-tuning trajectories recovers 91.1% of cross-run coordinates at 1500 steps versus 60.3% for endpoint matching, and the gain is not explained by merely routing through the base. The recovered gauge transfers tools that permutation-only alignment breaks: TinyLlama SAE reconstruction has NMSE 0.004 under $B_d$ versus 1.08 under $S_d$; Qwen sentiment steering preserves 95.8% of its effect versus 17.2%; refusal steering reverses sign under $S_d$; coordinate-preserving merges behave the same way. The same covariance governs stateful training: signed transport of AdamW state preserves the resumed trajectory, while permutation-only state follows a different one from a functionally identical checkpoint. Finally, gauge-sweep audits show index-level interpretability claims are reproducible only relative to an explicit gauge.

</details>


### [79] [Amplifying Membership Signal Through Chained Regeneration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31991)
*Wojciech Łapacz,Stanisław Pawlak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MADreMIA框架，利用迭代再生轨迹中记忆样本的高一致性和慢退化特性，在无需影子模型训练的情况下增强白盒、灰盒、黑盒的成员推断和数据集推断攻击，适用于图像、扩散、语言和音频模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型生成模型倾向于记忆训练数据，现有成员推断和数据集推断攻击依赖单次生成，信号弱且跨模态灵敏度有限；而训练影子模型对于大型生成模型往往不可行。

Method: 受模型自噬失调（MAD）现象启发，提出模型无关框架MADreMIA，通过链式迭代再生过程（每次输出作为下次输入）构建轨迹，利用记忆样本在迭代过程中更高的连贯性和更慢的退化程度作为推断信号。

Result: 在图像、扩散、语言模型上进行了全面评估，并在音频模型上展示了初步结果；该方法在低FPR条件下提供更丰富的成员证据，优于依赖单次生成的方法。

Conclusion: MADreMIA通过迭代轨迹利用固有信号，无需影子模型训练即可增强多模态、多访问设置下的隐私审计和版权执法能力。

Abstract: The tendency of large generative models to memorize training data makes sample verification critical for privacy auditing and copyright enforcement. Current membership (MIA) and dataset inference (DI) attacks often rely on one-shot generations, which yield weak signals and limited sensitivity across modalities. Inspired by Model Autophagy Disorder (MAD), we introduce MADreMIA, a model-agnostic framework that enhances white-, gray-, and black-box MIA and DI. Rather than relying on shadow model training -- often infeasible for large generative models -- our framework facilitates scalable inference by leveraging inherent signals through iterative trajectories. This process utilizes chained generations across diverse modalities, where each output serves as the subsequent input, to improve membership evidence at low FPR. We demonstrate that memorized training samples exhibit significantly higher coherence and slower degradation during iterative regeneration than non-member generations. Our results show that MADreMIA provides richer signals across diverse model families and modalities; we present comprehensive evaluations for IARs, diffusion, and language models, alongside preliminary results demonstrating its potential for audio models.

</details>


### [80] [Radial Suppression Accelerates Algorithmic Generalization: A Geometric Analysis of Delayed Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32000)
*Srijan Tiwari,Aditya Chauhan,Manjot Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出径向膨胀是导致神经网络在算法数据上先记忆后泛化（grokking）的关键因素，通过约束表征范数可加速泛化达6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经网络为何在算法任务上长期记忆数据后才突然泛化（grokking现象），揭示其背后的几何机制。

Method: 提出径向-角度分解的激活空间动力学分析框架，推导三个可检验命题；设计单超参数范数惩罚项，将激活软约束到sqrt(d)半径的超球面。

Result: 在模算术任务上，该方法使MLP和Transformer的grokking加速高达6倍；在10M参数的nanoGPT上进行3位数加法任务时，训练步数减半。

Conclusion: 径向膨胀驱动了记忆-泛化的延迟，抑制径向膨胀可促进各向异性、数据依赖的正则化，降低径向梯度能量，并引导收敛到更平坦的极小值，从而加速泛化。

Abstract: Why do neural networks memorize algorithmic training data long before they generalize? We present a geometric case study demonstrating that, on tasks where generalization requires discovering structured low-dimensional circuits, the memorization-generalization delay is driven by radial inflation of hidden representations under cross-entropy optimization. We formalize a radial-angular decomposition of activation-space dynamics and derive three testable propositions: (i) that penalizing radial inflation induces anisotropic, data-dependent weight regularization; (ii) that it suppresses radial gradient energy below the isotropic random baseline, forcing predominantly angular updates; and (iii) that it biases convergence toward flatter minima. To empirically validate these propositions, we study a single-hyperparameter norm penalty that softly constrains activations to a sqrt(d)-radius hypersphere. On modular arithmetic, this penalty accelerates grokking up to 6x across MLPs and Transformers, and halves training steps for a 10M-parameter nanoGPT on 3-digit addition.

</details>


### [81] [Surrogate Fidelity: When Can Open LLMs Explain Closed Ones?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32008)
*Philippe Chlenski,Zachariah Carmichael,Ayush Warikoo,Chia-Tse Shao,Yingxiao Ye,Aobo Yang,Vivek Miglani,Nehal Bandi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在无法获取闭源模型内部机制的情况下，能否利用开源模型的可解释性分析来推断闭源模型的行为。发现在二分类任务中，预测层面的一致性（模型给出相同答案）远高于归因层面的一致性（模型为何给出该答案），即预测保真度严重高估了归因保真度。白盒信号（注意力模式、扰动幅度）在不同模型间稳定但无法预测因果归因，而黑盒输入消融反而能捕捉因果归因。机制可解释性不能自动迁移到闭源目标模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性需要完全访问模型内部，但绝大多数广泛部署的语言模型API最多只暴露输出token的对数概率，这导致了一个代理问题：何时对开源模型的测量结果能让我们对闭源模型做出有效推断？

Method: 在预测、归因和表示三个层面评估替代保真度。针对二分类任务，使用对数几率作为API兼容的标量读出，采用留一法归因分析。在Llama、Qwen、GPT和Gemini四个系列共11个模型上进行实验。

Result: 预测保真度显著高估了归因保真度；存在"访问-有效性反转"现象：白盒信号（注意力模式、扰动幅度）在模型间高度稳定但对因果归因预测力弱，而黑盒输入消融反而能有效捕捉因果归因。

Conclusion: 机制性洞察不会自动迁移到闭源目标模型，预测层面的一致性不足以支持这种迁移。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability (MI) requires full access to model internals, yet the APIs for most widely deployed language models at best expose log-probabilities over output tokens. This creates a surrogate problem: when do measurements made on open models allow us to make claims about a closed model? We evaluate surrogate fidelity at the prediction, attribution, and representation levels. For binary classification tasks, log-odds provide an API-compatible scalar readout of the model's representation space, and leave-one-out attributions provide insight into model behavior. Across eleven models spanning four families (Llama, Qwen, GPT, and Gemini), we find that prediction fidelity substantially overstates attribution fidelity: models that agree on what the answer is often disagree on why. We document an access-validity inversion: white-box signals like attention patterns and perturbation magnitudes are highly stable across models but only weakly predictive of causal attributions, which black-box input ablations capture by design. Mechanistic insight does not automatically transfer to closed targets, and prediction-level agreement is insufficient to warrant such transfer. Code and results are available at https://github.com/facebookresearch/surrogate.

</details>


### [82] [CoMet: Context and Multiplicity Decomposition for Multimodal Uncertainty Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32012)
*Sanghyuk Chun,William Yang,Amaya Dharmasiri,Olga Russakovsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoMet，一种通过将不确定性分解为上下文特定项和多样性特定项来估计多模态大语言模型（MLLM）不确定性的方法，无需自回归生成或重复采样，在多个开放多模态基准测试中优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定性估计是AI模型的长期挑战，尤其在多模态大语言模型（MLLM）中，不确定性来源多样（不同模态及其关系）且输出空间无界，使得估计更加困难。现有方法效率低下，亟需高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出CoMet方法，将不确定性分解为上下文特定项（由任务或提示等上下文引起的歧义）和多样性特定项（上下文确定的合理答案与输入兼容的数量）。训练一个轻量级后验不确定性模块来估计这两个量，无需自回归生成答案或重复采样。

Result: 在开放多模态基准测试、幻觉检测和多选视觉问答基准测试上，CoMet在保持高效性的同时，持续优于现有不确定性估计基线方法。

Conclusion: CoMet通过分解不确定性来源并采用轻量级后验模块，实现了对MLLM高效且准确的不确定性估计，为多模态模型的可信度评估提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation has been a long-standing challenge in AI models; it amounts to "knowing what you don't know," and metacognition is notoriously difficult even for humans (cf. the Dunning-Kruger effect). Although it is still far from solved even in simpler classification systems, tackling it in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is becoming increasingly important. Within MLLMs, uncertainty can stem from any of the diverse sources as well as from their relationships, and further can stem from the unbounded answers in the open-ended setting. To tackle the issues, we propose CoMet, an MLLM uncertainty estimation method by decomposing uncertainty into a context-specific term and a multiplicity-specific term. The former captures ambiguity induced by the given context (e.g., task or prompt), while the latter captures how many plausible answers determined by the context remain compatible with the given input. We train a lightweight post-hoc uncertainty module to estimate these quantities, which enables efficient uncertainty estimation without autoregressive answer generation or repeated sampling. Experiments on various open-ended multimodal benchmarks, hallucination detection, and multiple-choice visual question answering benchmarks show that CoMet consistently improves uncertainty estimation over existing baselines while remaining efficient in practice. Code is available at https://github.com/princetonvisualai/comet_uncertainty

</details>


### [83] [FedLAB: Traceable Semantic Codebooks for Federated Multimodal Graph Foundation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32016)
*Zekai Chen,Kairui Yang,Xuaner Chen,Xunkai Li,Xun Wu,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedLAB，一种面向联邦多模态图基础模型的可溯源语义码本框架。通过构建分层码本（模态证据、节点语义、拓扑上下文），在保护隐私的前提下实现多模态图知识的可溯源表示学习，在10个基准和6个下游任务上比最优基线提升最高7.53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦多模态图学习方法通常通过参数、原型、嵌入或压缩码本交换知识，支持优化与迁移，但未显式揭示模态证据、节点语义和拓扑上下文如何共同支持预测，缺乏内在语义可溯性。

Method: 提出FedLAB框架：1) 构建类型化分层码本，分别建模模态证据、节点语义和拓扑上下文；2) 通过联邦语义质心预训练精炼可溯源单元；3) 保持原始多模态内容和图结构数据本地化，不共享原始数据。

Result: 在10个基准数据集和6个下游任务上，FedLAB相较于最优基线提升最高7.53%，并保持原生语义可溯源接口。

Conclusion: FedLAB通过可溯源的分层码本设计，在严格数据隔离条件下同时实现了多模态图知识的高效联邦学习与语义溯源，弥补了现有方法在可解释性方面的空白。

Abstract: Multimodal graph foundation models aim to learn reusable knowledge from graphs enriched with text, images, attributes, and relational topology, thereby supporting diverse graph-centric and modality-centric tasks. In practice, however, such multimodal graphs are often distributed across decentralized clients, where raw contents and local structures cannot be centrally shared due to privacy constraints. This motivates federated multimodal graph foundation learning, which requires not only transferable representation learning but also intrinsic semantic traceability under strict data isolation. Existing methods usually exchange or store knowledge through parameters, prototypes, embeddings, or compact codebooks, which support optimization and transfer but do not explicitly expose how modality evidence, node semantics, and topology context jointly support predictions. To bridge this gap, we propose FedLAB, a traceable semantic codebook framework that organizes multimodal graph knowledge into typed hierarchical codebooks for modality evidence, node semantics, and topology context. FedLAB further refines these trace units through federated semantic barycenter pre-training while keeping raw multimodal contents and graph structures local. Extensive experiments on 10 benchmarks and 6 downstream tasks show that FedLAB improves over state-of-the-art baselines by up to 7.53\%, while preserving a native semantic trace interface.

</details>


### [84] [TRIAGE: Role-Typed Credit Assignment for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32017)
*Yuanda Xu,Zhengze Zhou,Hejian Sang,Xiaomin Li,Jiaxin Zhang,Xinchen Du,Zhipeng Wang,Alborz Geramifard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRIAGE提出了一种基于语义角色标注的信用分配框架，通过将每个段分类为决定性进展、有用探索、无进展基础设施或回归，并应用固定的角色条件规则分配过程奖励，从而修正了标准GRPO仅依赖最终验证器结果进行信用分配的盲点。在ALFWorld、Search-QA和WebShop任务上，TRIAGE相较于GRPO提升了成功率，并减少了环境交互步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准GRPO对所有动作token使用统一的最终验证器结果作为advantage信号，这种结构不完整：对于失败的轨迹会惩罚有用的探索行为，对于成功的轨迹则会强化冗余或倒退动作。因此需要一种更精细化的信用分配机制来区分不同语义角色的动作。

Method: TRIAGE引入了一个结构化评判器，将每个段分类为四种语义角色之一（决定性进展、有用探索、无进展基础设施、回归），然后通过固定的角色条件规则将这些标签映射到有界的段级过程奖励。这种设计保持验证器结果作为优化方向来源，同时修正了仅依赖结果信用分配的两个主要盲点。文中还证明了角色条件信用是从角色标签本身可表达的最优段级修正项——即每段advantage残差在角色变量上的投影，从而在评判器可靠时降低advantage估计误差，并将此与低方差策略梯度相联系。

Result: 在ALFWorld、Search-QA和WebShop三个基准上，TRIAGE对于两种策略模型均提升了成功率，优于标量评判器推导的过程奖励和基于共享骨干网络的价值基线。消融实验表明，增益主要来自角色分类而非仅增加密集奖励：成功轨迹内部可靠地检测回归行为是主要贡献因素，探索信用提供了一致的次要增益。对于已完成轨迹，TRIAGE在ALFWorld和WebShop上分别额外减少了10.4%和14.8%的环境交互轮数。

Conclusion: TRIAGE通过角色类型化的信用分配框架有效改进了GRPO的信用分配机制，在多个智能体推理任务上取得了更好的性能与更高的效率。这种基于语义角色的过程奖励设计是一种原理性的、低方差的advantage估计修正方法。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning requires assigning credit to environment-facing actions such as searches, clicks, edits, navigation commands, and object interactions. Standard GRPO uses the final verifier outcome as a uniform advantage over all action tokens. This outcome signal is useful but structurally incomplete: it punishes useful exploration in failed rollouts and reinforces redundant or regressive actions in successful rollouts. We propose TRIAGE, a role-typed credit assignment framework that adds a semantic role axis to outcome credit. A structured judge classifies each segment as decisive progress, useful exploration, no-progress infrastructure, or regression, and a fixed role-conditioned rule maps these labels to bounded segment-level process rewards. This keeps verifier outcomes as the source of optimization direction while correcting the two main blind spots of outcome-only credit. We further show that role-conditioned credit is the optimal segment-level correction expressible from role labels alone -- a projection of the per-segment advantage residual onto the role variable -- so that the fixed role constants reduce advantage estimation error whenever the judge is reliable, and we connect this to lower-variance policy gradients. Across ALFWorld, Search-QA, and WebShop, TRIAGE improves success rates over GRPO for two policy models and outperforms both a scalar judge-derived process reward and an outcome-supervised shared-backbone value baseline. Ablations show that the gain comes from role typing rather than merely adding dense rewards: reliable detection of regression inside successful trajectories is the dominant contributor, while exploration credit provides a consistent secondary gain; on completed ALFWorld and WebShop rollouts, TRIAGE also reduces environment-facing turns by an additional $10.4\%$ and $14.8\%$ relative to GRPO.

</details>


### [85] [SemRF: A Semantic Reference Frame for Residual-Stream Dynamics in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32022)
*Jian Gu,Aldeida Aleti,Chunyang Chen,Hongyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出语义参考系（SemRF）形式化框架，将语言模型中的语义测量与残差动力学分离。通过锚点固定、伪逆绑定和Voronoi图分析，将逐层残差计算转化为深度方向的语义轨迹，并定义管状约束下的最小作用量路径（样条曲线），建立轨迹复杂度与参数效率之间的条件性联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有残差流分析中，嵌入锚点与去嵌入读出在不同层间可能对所选跨度存在不一致，导致观测到的运动可能反映的是测量漂移而非真实计算。为区分语义测量与残差动力学，需要一种锚点对齐的形式化方法。

Method: 1) 提出语义参考系（SemRF），固定锚点并基于锚点测量状态；2) 通过伪逆绑定实现精确同步，在受限双可逆性下获得稳定语义基坐标；3) 利用锚点诱导语义Voronoi图，以距离/logits将各层分配到粗粒度细胞；4) 定义Voronoi迹、松弛管状约束及管内最小作用量路径，该路径唯一且满足离散样条方程；5) 通过参数到轨迹映射建立轨迹复杂度与参数效率的条件性联系。

Result: 在受限双可逆性条件下，SemRF可提供稳定的语义基坐标、畸变界和近似恒等的变化。最小作用量路径在正二次权重下唯一存在，低曲率意味着分段线性可压缩性和局部知识密度。轨迹复杂度越低，语义结点越少，参数效率越高。保证条件依赖于可控的接口误差和显式管状约束下的小投影残差。

Conclusion: SemRF为语言模型中间解码提供了一种锚点对齐的测量框架，将残差计算解释为深度方向的语义轨迹，并通过Voronoi迹和最小作用量路径揭示了轨迹复杂度与参数效率之间的条件性联系。

Abstract: Residual-stream analysis asks how language-model computation evolves across depth, but intermediate decoding requires comparable readout coordinates across layers. If embedding anchors and unembedding readout disagree on the chosen span, apparent motion may reflect measurement drift rather than computation. We introduce \emph{Semantic Reference Frames} (SemRF), an anchor-based formalism separating semantic measurement from residual dynamics. A SemRF fixes anchors and measures states against them. Pseudo-inverse tying gives exact synchronization; under restricted bi-invertibility, SemRF yields stable semantic-basis coordinates, distortion bounds, and near-identity changes. With the frame fixed, residual computation becomes a depthwise semantic trajectory. The anchors induce a semantic Voronoi diagram: distance, or evidence such as logits, assigns each layer to a coarse cell, while coordinates retain within-cell motion and margins. We define layerwise steps, contribution profiles, and imbalance diagnostics, then use the Voronoi trace to define a margin-relaxed tube. The canonical trace is the minimum-action path inside this tube; when nonempty with positive quadratic weight, it is unique and obeys a discrete spline equation away from active constraints. Excess action controls step, curvature, and profile mismatch. Low curvature implies piecewise-linear compressibility and local knowledge density: lower trace complexity means fewer semantic knots. Through the parameter-to-trajectory map, this gives a conditional link to parameter efficiency: among admissible settings fitting data, lower-action and lower-complexity traces use fewer semantic degrees of freedom. The guarantees require controlled interface error and small projection residual under explicit tube constraints.

</details>


### [86] [AdaJEPA: An Adaptive Latent World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32026)
*Ying Wang,Oumayma Bounou,Yann LeCun,Mengye Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaJEPA提出了一个自适应潜在世界模型，在模型预测控制闭环中进行测试时自适应，通过单步梯度更新即可显著提升规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统潜在世界模型在测试时被冻结，当预测不准确时（尤其在测试时分布偏移下）会导致规划失败，缺乏适应能力。

Method: 提出AdaJEPA，在MPC闭环中执行测试时自适应：规划并执行首个动作块，利用观测到的下一状态转换作为自监督信号更新模型，然后重新规划。

Result: 在多种目标到达任务中，仅需每个MPC重规划步骤进行一次梯度更新，即可显著提升规划成功率。

Conclusion: 闭环更新能持续重新校准世界模型，无需额外专家演示，有效应对测试时分布偏移。

Abstract: Latent world models enable planning from high-dimensional observations by predicting future states in a compact latent space. However, these models are typically kept frozen at test time: when their predictions become inaccurate, planning can fail, especially under test-time distribution shift. To address this, we propose AdaJEPA, an adaptive latent world model that performs test-time adaptation within the closed loop of model predictive control (MPC). After training, AdaJEPA plans and executes the first action chunk, uses the observed next-state transition as a self-supervised adaptation signal, and replans with the updated model. This closed-loop update continuously recalibrates the world model without additional expert demonstrations. Across a range of goal-reaching tasks, AdaJEPA substantially improves planning success with as few as one gradient step per MPC replanning step.

</details>


### [87] [QVal: Cheaply Evaluating Dense Supervision Signals for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32034)
*Sergio Hernández-Gutiérrez,Matteo Merler,Ilze Amanda Auzina,Joschka Strüber,Ameya Prabhu,Matthias Bethge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QVal，一个无需训练的测试平台，用于直接评估密集监督信号的质量（Q对齐性），而非通过下游训练性能。对21种密集监督方法的基准测试显示，简单的提示基线方法 consistently 优于近期文献中的复杂方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的密集监督方法评估依赖于下游训练性能，这种方式昂贵、混淆了信号质量与训练工程因素，且不同方法因训练设置不同而难以公平比较。

Method: 提出QVal测试平台，通过测量密集监督方法给出的分数是否与强参考策略的Q值排序一致（Q对齐性）来直接评估信号质量，无需训练过程。

Result: 在4个环境、7个方法家族、21种密集监督方法、6个模型上进行了1200+实验，发现简单的提示基线方法 consistently 优于近期文献中的密集监督方法，且性能按方法家族聚类。

Conclusion: QVal提供了一种训练无关的评估方式，能够公平比较不同密集监督方法，揭示简单基线方法的优越性，并为未来密集监督方法的研究提供了可扩展的基准。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly act over long horizons, where a single trajectory can contain hundreds or thousands of actions. In these settings, outcome-only rewards provide too sparse guidance, failing to inform the model about the goodness of intermediate actions. Dense supervision methods aim to solve this problem by scoring intermediate steps, from intrinsic confidence to self-distillation and embedding similarities. However, it is common practice to evaluate them by measuring the downstream performance of a training pipeline that integrates them. This is expensive, conflates supervision quality with training engineering confounders, and renders different methodological families requiring distinct training setups incomparable. As a result, dense supervision methods are rarely benchmarked on common ground. We introduce QVal, a training-free testbed for directly evaluating dense supervision signals. Given a state-action pair, QVal measures how well a method's score is Q-aligned: whether it orders actions according to the Q-values of a strong reference-policy. This lets us compare signals before any training run and separate signal quality from other engineering choices. We instantiate QVal as QVal-v1.0, benchmarking 21 dense supervision methods across four diverse environments and seven methodological families, with over 1.2K evaluation experiments across six open-weight model backbones. We find that simple prompting baselines consistently outperform recent dense supervision methods from the literature, and that performance clusters strongly by family. These findings hold across model sizes, environments, and observation modalities. QVal is designed to be easily extensible to new environments and methods, enabling researchers to iterate on dense supervision methods before any training run.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [88] [What Drives Interactive Improvement from Feedback?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30774)
*Bartłomiej Cupiał,Jan Łojek,Mikołaj Garstecki,Szymon Pobłocki,Alicja Ziarko,Piotr Miłoś*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过受控的学生-教师评估协议，研究了自然语言反馈是否能带来超越重复尝试的改进。实验发现，多轮提升往往不能证明反馈的有效性：自生成反馈几乎不带来额外增益，而强外部教师能产生显著的反馈特异性提升。交互改进更多取决于学生利用反馈的能力而非教师身份，但固定学生下教师选择仍然重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮语言智能体交互中，更高的最终准确率可能来自有用反馈，但也可能来自重新采样、格式修正或额外的测试时计算。为了区分这些因素，需要分离出反馈带来的真实改进。

Method: 提出受控的学生-教师评估协议，在Omni-MATH、Codeforces、BBEH Linguini和ARC-AGI1四个基准上，使用13个开源模型作为学生和教师角色，比较外部反馈、自我反馈和无引导自我精炼三种设置，同时变化交互历史、任务难度和教师获取任务特权信息的条件。

Result: 多轮改进通常不能证明反馈的有效使用；自生成反馈相比无引导自我精炼几乎无额外收益；强外部教师能产生显著更大的反馈特异性增益；交互改进更多由学生利用反馈的能力驱动而非教师身份，但固定学生时教师选择仍然重要。

Conclusion: 基于反馈的智能体评估应设置重复尝试基线；利用反馈的能力（而非仅仅反馈的可获得性）是交互改进的核心瓶颈。

Abstract: We study when natural-language feedback produces improvement beyond the gains obtainable from repeated attempts alone. In multi-turn language agent setting, higher final accuracy can reflect useful feedback, but it can also arise from resampling, format correction, or additional test-time computation. To separate these effects, we introduce a controlled student-teacher protocol across Omni-MATH, Codeforces, BBEH Linguini, and ARC-AGI1, evaluating thirteen open-weight models in both student and teacher roles. We compare external feedback, self-feedback, and unguided self-refinement, while varying interaction history, task difficulty, and teacher access to privileged task information. Across settings, we find that multi-turn improvement is often not evidence of feedback use: self-generated feedback adds little beyond unguided self-refinement, whereas the strongest external teachers produce substantially larger feedback-specific gains, suggesting that useful feedback must provide guidance beyond generic retry. Dense student-teacher interaction matrices further show that interactive gains are driven more by the student's ability to use feedback than by the teacher's identity, although teacher choice remains important for a fixed student. These results suggest that feedback-based agents should be evaluated against repeated-attempt baselines, and that ability to act on feedback, not merely feedback availability, is a central bottleneck for interactive improvement. We release our controlled student-teacher evaluation framework at https://j-lojek.github.io/feedback-generation-is-a-bottleneck/.

</details>


### [89] [Contrastive Reflection for Iterative Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30840)
*Derek Koh,Jinghui Mo,Benjamin H. Le,Jiening Zhan,Baofen Zheng,Kevin Bevis,Nathaniel C. Owen,Lauren Elizabeth Charney,Wenqiong Liu,Jingwei Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出对比反思框架，通过错误锚定的行为切片和邻近成功示例进行对比，由教师LLM生成定向提示编辑，并通过验证性能筛选改进，在HotpotQA上将准确率从51.4%提升至60.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理的提示优化在信息检索应用中更像调试而非盲搜索，工程师需要知道哪个行为失败、哪个邻近行为仍有效、二者的区别，以及编辑是否会引入回归。现有方法缺少这种可检查的对比分析机制。

Method: 提出对比反思（Contrastive Reflection）迭代框架：基于任务中心的质量定义，从QA代理的检索/推理轨迹和评分代理的维度级评分与理由中识别错误锚定的行为切片，添加同一区域的邻近成功示例，由教师LLM提出定向提示编辑，仅在验证性能提升时接受变更，可选进行回归检查。

Result: 在HotpotQA检索增强QA任务上，单次对比修复将精确匹配准确率从51.4%提升至60.4%；仅用失败样本或随机证据的变体改进较小且破坏更多已正确示例；与MIPROv2（59.4%）和GEPA（57.0%）相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 对比反思是一种可解释、可检查的提示优化循环，使IR代理的提示修复更加验证驱动，核心贡献是对比反思循环本身而非具体的切片选择器。

Abstract: LLM agents are becoming central to information retrieval: they issue retrieval queries, synthesize answers, and increasingly serve as judges for IR evaluation. Improving the prompts that control these agents is an optimization problem, but in applied IR settings it often looks less like blind search and more like debugging. Engineers need to know which behavior failed, which nearby behavior still worked, what distinguishes the two, and whether a prompt edit improves held-out quality without introducing regressions.
  We present Contrastive Reflection, an iterative prompt-optimization framework for agentic IR workflows. The framework starts from a task-centric quality definition: QA agents expose retrieval or reasoning traces, and grading agents expose dimension-level scores and rationales. These structured traces are used to identify error-anchored behavioral slices, add nearby successful examples from the same region, and ask a Teacher LLM to propose a targeted prompt edit. Candidate edits are accepted only when validation performance improves, optionally subject to regression checks. We instantiate the framework with a tree-based slice selector, but the contribution is the contrastive reflection loop rather than the tree itself.
  On a public HotpotQA retrieval-augmented QA setup, one tree-selected contrastive repair improves held-out exact-match accuracy from 51.4% to 60.4%. Failure-only and random-evidence variants improve less and break more previously correct examples. A light instruction-only comparison places the method near modern prompt optimizers: MIPROv2 reaches 59.4% and GEPA 57.0%. The result is an interpretable optimization loop for IR agents, aimed at making prompt repair more inspectable and validation-driven.

</details>


### [90] [How Can AI Find My Model? A Model-Finding Experimental Study Considering Data Formats, Embeddings, and Retrieval Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30846)
*Jhon G. Botello,Jose J. Padilla,Erika Frydenlund,Krzysztof Rechowicz,Eric Weisel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了数据表示、Transformer嵌入模型和检索策略对基于自然语言查询的仿真模型发现性能的影响，结果表明数据表示方式至关重要，开源嵌入模型能达到高性能，且重排序方法对复杂查询尤为重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真模型复用中，从大量共存模型中识别与建模意图一致的模型仍然困难，需要利用AI检索技术在这一语义层面进行操作。

Method: 基于检索的方法，实验研究数据表示、Transformer嵌入模型和检索策略的影响，使用recall@5和nDCG@5等标准信息检索指标评估多种查询类型的性能。

Result: 数据表示方式影响显著；开源嵌入模型能达到高性能；重排序方法在查询复杂度增加时尤为重要。

Conclusion: 该工作为AI驱动的模型发现提供了基线，并讨论了其在推动AI驱动的可组合性和互操作性方面的作用。

Abstract: Discovering simulation models for reuse remains a fundamental challenge in Modeling and Simulation (M&S). When many models coexist, identifying those that align with a given modeling intent remains difficult. Recent advances in Artificial Intelligence (AI), particularly retrieval-based approaches, offer a promising pathway to operate at this semantic layer. In this paper, we present an experimental study investigating the impact of data representation, transformer-based embedding models, and retrieval strategies on the discovery of simulation models using natural language queries. We evaluated performance across multiple query types using standard information retrieval metrics, including recall@5 and nDCG@5. Results show that data representation matters, open-source embedding models can achieve high performance, and reranking methods are important, especially as query complexity increases. This work provides a baseline for AI-driven model discovery and discusses its role in advancing toward AI-driven composability and interoperability.

</details>


### [91] [BayesBench: Evaluating LLM Belief Trajectories Under Multi-Turn Evidence Accumulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30850)
*Ankur Samanta,Akshayaa Magesh,Tal Lancewicki,Ayush Jain,Youliang Yu,Paul Sajda,Kaveh Hassani,Aditya Modi,Daniel R. Jiang,Yonathan Efroni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BayesBench基准，评估大语言模型在多轮对话中是否像贝叶斯理性推理者一样更新信念。实验发现，模型规模增大能改善潜在变量推断，但无法可靠地将这些推断转化为理性预测，存在"推断-预测"鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估仅关注模型在单轮格式下的最终回答，忽略了多轮对话中模型应随证据积累逐步更新信念这一关键过程。作者希望系统性地检验LLMs的信念更新是否接近贝叶斯理性推理者。

Method: 构建BayesBench基准套件，包含三个渐进复杂任务：(i)贝叶斯估计——从序列证据中推断未知参数；(ii)贝叶斯预测——将推断的潜在信念转化为结果预测；(iii)潜在框架贝叶斯预测——通过用户角色框架过滤观测，需联合推断潜在状态与角色。在7个LLM（3B-70B）上进行评估。

Result: 模型规模增大改善了潜在变量推断和证据累积，部分更新接近贝叶斯后验。但这些提升不能可靠地传递到下游预测任务，暴露出推断潜在结构与将其用于理性更新目标信念之间的差距。

Conclusion: LLMs在多轮设置中存在"推断-预测"鸿沟：即使能正确推断潜在结构，也无法将其用于理性信念更新。这表明当前模型在推理的完整链条上存在断裂，需要进一步研究如何弥合这一差距。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are typically deployed in multi-turn conversations, where each turn provides new evidence that should reduce epistemic uncertainty about their environment. Acting rationally then requires inferring the unobserved quantities that govern it and updating beliefs about them as evidence accumulates. Yet most evaluations only score the model's final-turn answer in a single-turn format, leaving this process unexamined. We ask how closely LLMs' belief updates match those of a rational Bayesian reasoner in multi-turn settings, and introduce BayesBench, a suite of simulation environments that probe this across three progressively complex tasks: (i) Bayesian estimation, where the model infers an unknown parameter from sequential evidence; (ii) Bayesian prediction, where the model turns inferred beliefs about a latent variable into outcome forecasts; and (iii) latent-framed Bayesian prediction, where observations are filtered through a user-persona framing, requiring joint inference over the latent state and the persona. Across seven LLMs (3B--70B), scaling improves latent inference and evidence accumulation, with updates occasionally matching the Bayesian posterior. However, these gains do not reliably carry over to downstream prediction, exposing a gap between inferring latent structure and using it to rationally update beliefs about the target outcome.

</details>


### [92] [When Does Learning to Stop Help? A Cost-Aware Study of Early Exits in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30852)
*Zhe Dong,Fang Qin,Manish Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究推理语言模型的早停策略，提出LearnStop方法，通过在线特征预测前缀正确性。实验发现，学习式停止在自由形式数学任务上优于固定预算和标量退出，但在多项选择和极难任务上标量规则更具竞争力。学习式停止并非通用替代方案，其价值取决于轨迹结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型在不同实例上的有用计算量不同，但尚不清楚学习式停止规则在何时优于简单的置信度或收敛阈值，因此需要系统研究这一问题。

Method: 提出LearnStop方法，这是一种无隐藏状态的检查点停止器。在固定预算检查点处，从当前推理前缀中探测短答案，并利用答案置信度、熵、前缀投票份额、答案稳定性和回溯标记密度等在线特征预测前缀正确性。

Result: 在18个任务-模型设置下，学习式停止的效果因任务而异。在自由形式数学任务上（如GSM8K+Qwen3-32B），学习式多特征停止改进了固定预算前沿，后验峰值自适应增益达+0.157，验证选择操作点保持正增益，相对于最强标量基线的配对增益为+0.028。在多项选择和极难设置下，标量置信度、熵或稳定性规则具有竞争力或更强。

Conclusion: 学习式停止并非标量退出的通用替代方案，而是取决于轨迹结构的工具。当许多问题在达到完整预算前已正确但缺乏单一可靠标量停止信号时，学习式停止有用；当置信度或答案收敛已能解决问题时，其收益基本消失。

Abstract: Reasoning models spend different amounts of useful computation across instances, but it remains unclear when a learned stopping rule improves over simple confidence or convergence thresholds. We study this question with LearnStop, a hidden-state-free checkpoint stopper for reasoning language models. At fixed budget checkpoints, LearnStop probes a short answer from the current reasoning prefix and predicts prefix correctness from online features such as answer confidence, entropy, prefix vote share, answer stability, and backtracking-marker density. Across 18 task-model settings spanning GSM8K, MATH-500, MMLU-Pro, AIME-90, GPQA, Qwen3, and DeepSeek-R1 distillations, the answer is task-dependent. On free-form math, learned multi-feature stopping improves the fixed-budget frontier and often beats scalar exits: on GSM8K with Qwen3-32B, the empirical frontier reaches a post-hoc peak adapt gain of +0.157, validation-selected operating points preserve positive gains, and the paired gain over the strongest scalar baseline is +0.028. On multiple-choice and very hard settings, scalar confidence, entropy, or stability rules are competitive or stronger. We therefore frame learned stopping not as a universal replacement for scalar exits, but as a tool whose value depends on trajectory structure. We further provide validation-selected operating points, paired bootstrap tests, finite-grid lost-correct risk calibration, cost accounting under KV-fork, prefix-cache, and black-box regimes, H100 serving profiles, checkpoint-schedule sweeps, transfer analyses, and robustness checks. The main practical finding is that learned stopping is useful when many questions become correct before full budget but do not exhibit a single reliable scalar stopping signal; its benefits largely disappear when confidence or answer convergence already solves the stopping problem.

</details>


### [93] [Beyond expert users: agents should help users construct preferences, not just elicit them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30863)
*Irena Saracay,Ludwig Schmidt,Carlos Guestrin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出用户并非总是具有明确偏好的专家，引入CoPref模型和CoShop基准测试，发现前沿AI代理在帮助用户学习领域知识以形成偏好方面表现不佳，准确率不超过56%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统代理系统假设用户具有完善偏好，但现实中的用户往往缺乏领域知识，无法在未获帮助的情况下表达偏好。作者旨在形式化这一认知缺口，将信息经济学中的搜索-体验-信任品框架引入人机交互领域。

Method: 基于信息经济学的Search-Experience-Credence框架，提出CoPref偏好构建模型；构建CoShop交互式推荐基准测试环境，评估五个前沿模型在五轮对话中的推荐准确率。

Result: 五个前沿模型在CoShop上最高准确率仅为56%；失败根源不在于商品检索能力，而在于对话交互未能有效扩展用户对自身需求的认识。

Conclusion: 当前AI代理在帮助用户获取领域知识以形成偏好方面存在系统性缺陷，需重新设计交互策略以支持用户的偏好构建过程，而非仅依赖预设偏好假设。

Abstract: Agents typically assume an expert user -- one with well-formed preferences about what they want -- and default to clarifying questions whenever the task is underspecified. We argue this assumption is unrealistic. Users often lack the domain knowledge to have completely specified preferences; if asked about their preference on some feature, the user may be unable to answer without the agent helping the user to learn some domain knowledge needed to form a preference for that feature, e.g., via examples or explanations. To formalize these principles, we draw on the Search-Experience-Credence framework from Information Economics to introduce CoPref, a model of how users construct preferences based on agent dialog actions. We then study these ideas concretely in agentic recommender systems, proposing CoShop, an interactive benchmark. In CoShop, an agent converses with and makes recommendations for a CoPref user. The agent's performance depends on whether it can help the user gain the knowledge needed to specify the task well. Evaluating five frontier models, we find that no agent exceeds 56% accuracy on CoShop despite five turns of interaction. Failures stem not from agents' ability to find items, but from how little the interaction expands what users know about what they want.

</details>


### [94] [Investigating Multi-Agent Deliberation in Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30906)
*Cor Steging,Ludi van Leeuwen,Tadeusz Zbiegień*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索了基于大语言模型的多智能体 deliberation 方法在法律推理任务中的应用，提出了两种受法庭程序和法律论证启发的新型多智能体框架。实验表明，多智能体框架与基线大模型整体性能相当，但输出结果显著不同，且能成功解决基线无法处理的问题，尤其在需要多角度批判性思考的场景下表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律领域的人工智能应用具有提升司法可及性的潜力，但基于大语言模型的自主智能体（尤其是多智能体方法）在法律推理任务中的研究尚属空白。作者旨在探索多智能体 deliberation 方法在法律推理中的有效性。

Method: 提出两种受法庭程序和法律论证启发的新型多智能体 deliberation 框架；在法律和非法律基准任务上进行实验；通过与基线大模型对比进行定量和定性评估。

Result: 多智能体框架与基线大模型整体性能相当，但输出结果显著不同；能成功解决基线模型无法解决的问题，反之亦然；在需要多角度批判性思考的任务中表现更优。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统是人工智能在法律领域的一个有前景的方向，受法律启发的多智能体 deliberation 方法在推理任务中展现出潜力。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence is increasingly applied to the field of law, and has the potential to increase access to justice. One particular movement that is gaining traction is that of agentic AI, wherein AI agents, based on Large Language Models (LLMs) can take autonomous actions. In particular, multi-agent approaches in the legal domain remain largely unexplored. In this paper, we investigate multi-agent deliberation methods for legal reasoning tasks using LLMs. We explore multi-agent deliberation (MAD) and introduce two novel multi-agent frameworks inspired by courtroom procedures and legal argumentation. Our experiments on both legal and non-legal benchmarks reveal that multi-agent frameworks achieve comparable overall performance to baseline large language models, but produce significantly distinct answers. Notably, these approaches can successfully solve cases that the baseline fails to address, and vice versa. We conduct a qualitative evaluation and highlight scenarios where multi-agent frameworks outperform monolithic approaches. For example, multi-agent approaches appear better suited for answering questions that require critical thinking from multiple perspectives. Our work positions multi-agent systems as a promising direction for AI in the legal domain, while demonstrating the potential of law-inspired multi-agent approaches for deliberation.

</details>


### [95] [Why Solve It Twice? Hierarchical Accumulation of Skills for Transfer-Efficient ML Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30911)
*Yongbin Kim,Yashar Talebirad,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HASTE是一个分层多智能体系统，通过三级知识体系（全局、领域、竞赛特定）组织跨竞赛知识，实现知识复用，在MLE-Bench Lite基准上达到77.3%奖牌率，冷启动与热启动实验表明分层知识加载显著优于平面加载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML工程智能体每次竞赛都从零开始，浪费计算资源重复发现已知技术，缺乏跨竞赛知识复用机制。

Method: 提出HASTE分层多智能体系统，将跨竞赛知识组织为全局、领域、竞赛特定三个范围层级，每个层级匹配对应的智能体层级；通过编排器协调领域专家，并利用LLM驱动的抽象机制促进层级间学习迁移。

Result: 控制消融实验中，层级加载在8个竞赛中达到100%奖牌率，平面加载仅62.5%（与无技能加载相同）且消耗2倍输出token；在MLE-Bench Lite完整基准上达到77.3%奖牌率；热启动减少52%的精化迭代次数，提案保留率从42%（低库存）提升至85%（50+技能）。

Conclusion: 更好的知识组织可以在一定程度上替代模型强度和计算预算，HASTE的分层知识管理为ML工程智能体提供了高效的跨竞赛知识复用范式。

Abstract: ML engineering agents waste compute rediscovering known techniques because every competition is a cold start. We present HASTE, a hierarchical multi-agent system that organizes cross-competition knowledge into three scope tiers (global, domain, and competition-specific), each coupled to a matching agent level. An orchestrator coordinates domain specialists and promotes learning between tiers via LLM-driven abstraction. A controlled ablation provides evidence for scoped loading: holding a 159-skill inventory constant across 8 competitions, tiered loading achieves a 100% medal rate while flat loading reaches only 62.5%, the same medal rate as loading no skills, and consumes 2x the output tokens. On the full MLE-Bench Lite benchmark (22 Kaggle competitions), HASTE reaches a medal rate of 77.3% using Claude Sonnet 4.6 at 12h per competition. In a cold-start run, the system begins with no accumulated skills. In warm-start runs, it reloads skills learned from earlier competitions, using only global and domain-level skills for transfer across competitions. Warm starts use 52% fewer refinement iterations, and the fraction of proposed changes kept by the agent rises from 42% at low inventory to 85% once 50+ skills are available. These results suggest that better knowledge organization can partly substitute for model strength and compute budget in ML-engineering agents.

</details>


### [96] [RoPoLL: Robust Panel of LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30931)
*Anish Acharya,Kris W Pan,Brian Verkhovsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化LLM陪审团（PoLL）为Huber污染模型，证明其在任何正污染下偏差无界；提出RoPoLL，用几何中位数替代均值聚合，实现最优有限样本断裂点1/2，在13个裁判模型和四个腐败场景下显著优于PoLL，3个裁判（38B）即可超越Mistral-Large-3（675B）1.31倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Jury（PoLL）作为多评委共识评估范式已被广泛使用，但其统计行为（尤其是面对评委偏见时的鲁棒性）缺乏理论分析，现有方法对模式坍缩、谄媚、安全拒绝等LLM典型偏差高度敏感。

Method: 将PoLL形式化为Huber污染模型，证明其偏差无界；提出RoPoLL，保留PoLL评委组但将聚合函数替换为几何中位数（鲁棒均值估计），给出有限样本误差界与信息论极小化下界。

Result: 在13个开源裁判模型、3个奖励模型基准、4种腐败场景下，RoPoLL在所有有偏污染上主导PoLL：跨维度攻击提升约19%，对重尾拜占庭攻击提升数个数量级；3裁判RoPoLL（38B）在30%双模态随机污染下以1.31x超越675B的Mistral-Large-3，实现18倍参数优势。

Conclusion: RoPoLL通过鲁棒均值估计解决了PoLL对评委偏差的脆弱性问题，理论最优且实践有效，但存在统计-计算差距（多项式时间RoPoLL相对于难解的Tukey半空间中位数需付出sqrt(d)代价）。

Abstract: The LLM Jury, a Panel of LLM Evaluators (PoLL) reporting consensus scores, has become a practical alternative to single-judge LLM evaluation, yet its statistical behavior remains poorly understood. We formalize the LLM Jury under the Huber contamination model and show that PoLL incurs unbounded bias
  under any positive contamination, regardless of jury size, whenever a single judge fails in a biased, LLM-typical way (mode collapse, sycophancy, safety refusal). Framing jury consensus as classical robust mean estimation, we propose RoPoLL (Robust Panel of LLM-as-Judge), which preserves the PoLL
  panel but replaces the aggregation function with a robust mean estimator, instantiated with the geometric median (GM): tuning-free, with the optimal finite-sample breakdown point 1/2. A finite-sample error bound and a matching information-theoretic minimax lower bound agree on the parametric rate
  sigma*sqrt(d/N) and differ on the breakdown floor by a factor of sqrt(d), a statistical-computational gap that polynomial-time RoPoLL pays relative to the intractable Tukey halfspace median. Across 13 open-weight judges (4B-675B), three reward-model benchmarks, and four corruption regimes at rates up
  to 50%, RoPoLL dominates PoLL on every biased corruption type: by about 19% on cross-dimensional attacks at matched compute, and by orders of magnitude on heavy-tailed Byzantine adversaries. A 3-judge RoPoLL committee at 38B beats Mistral-Large-3 (675B) by 1.31x on HelpSteer-2 under 30% bimodal-random
  corruption, an 18x parameter advantage at better accuracy; a Noisy-GT control confirms the premium is paid against biased contamination, not benign imprecision.

</details>


### [97] [AgRefactor: Self-Evolving Agentic Workflow for HLS Compatibility and Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30949)
*Yang Zou,Zijian Ding,Yizhou Sun,Jason Cong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgRefactor是一个基于LLM的多智能体工作流，用于将软件代码重构为HLS兼容程序。它结合自进化记忆系统和自动化重构工具，在11个基准测试中的9个上达到或超越现有方法，并实现6.51x的几何平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将真实世界软件转换为可综合的HLS代码具有挑战性：现有语言支持受限、软硬件编程实践存在鸿沟，而现有自动化方法和基于LLM的重构方法灵活性不足、难以扩展且计算成本高昂。

Method: AgRefactor采用LLM驱动的多智能体工作流，核心创新包括：(1)自进化记忆系统，跨任务积累和检索事实性与策略性知识；(2)集成自动化重构工具，平衡LLM驱动的重写与高效的基于工具的转换，以降低成本并提升可扩展性。

Result: 在11个具有挑战性的真实世界基准测试（长度是先前工作中最复杂案例的5-10倍）中的9个上，AgRefactor超越或匹配了最先进的自动化重构工具和强LLM基线。进一步性能优化实现比SoTA调优工具6.51x的几何平均加速，比优化过的开源设计快1.20x，额外资源开销少于20%。

Conclusion: AgRefactor是完全自动化且开源的系统，通过多智能体协作与记忆系统有效解决了软件到HLS代码重构的挑战，在复杂真实场景中取得了显著性能提升和效率优势。

Abstract: High-Level Synthesis (HLS) provides a fast path from concepts to silicon, but converting real-world software into synthesizable HLS code remains challenging due to restrictive language support and the gap between software and hardware programming practices. Existing automated and LLM-based refactoring approaches partially address this problem, yet they often lack flexibility, struggle to scale, and incur high computational costs. We introduce AgRefactor, an LLM-based multi-agent workflow for refactoring software into HLS-compatible programs. AgRefactor incorporates a self-evolving memory system that accumulates and retrieves factual and strategic knowledge across tasks, improving robustness and efficiency on unseen programs. To reduce cost and enhance scalability, it integrates automated refactoring tools, enabling agents to balance LLM-driven rewrites with efficient tool-based transformations. On 9 out of 11 challenging real-world benchmarks, which are 5-10x longer than the most complex cases studied in prior work, AgRefactor outperforms or matches the state-of-the-art automated refactoring tool and a strong LLM-based baseline built on the same framework backbone. Further agentic performance optimization yields a 6.51x geometric mean speedup over the SoTA pragma tuning tool and a 1.20x speedup over optimized open-source designs with less than 20% extra resources. AgRefactor is fully-automated and open-sourced.

</details>


### [98] [Neuro-Bayesian-Symbolic Residual Attention Shallow Network: Explainable Deep Learning for Cybersecurity Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30953)
*Nicolaie Popescu-Bodorin,Madeleine Togher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种神经-贝叶斯-符号混合浅层网络（NBS-RASN），仅用12层80个可解释神经元实现可解释的网络安全风险评估，通过硬约束的认知公理门控机制和残差注意力循环赋予深层推理能力，在20个开源项目上达到0.79-0.97置信度，证明了浅层网络结合深度推理可超越不透明深度模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型在网络安全风险评估中牺牲可解释性换取准确性，而高风险决策场景（如开源生态安全）迫切需要可解释的评估方法，因此需要一种既能保持高表现力又能保证完全可解释的混合架构。

Method: 设计浅层神经网络（80个可解释神经元，12层），将领域知识、因果推理和专家判断编码为可微组件；引入门控机制强制执行五项认知公理（精确性、因果性、可证伪性、透明性、完备性）作为硬约束；通过残差注意力与反馈循环实现深层学习特性；输出可完全分解的确定性加权分数与专家调整项，每项调整可溯源至命名的放大器因子。

Result: 在覆盖OWASP Top 10:2025所有类别和语言风险类的20个开源项目上进行验证，达到0.79-0.97的置信度分数，可解释性由设计保证而非训练算法事后提供。

Conclusion: 挑战了深度学习必须依赖深层网络的传统假设，证明浅层网络结合深层推理在高风险网络安全场景中可超越不透明模型，可解释性在关键决策中至关重要。

Abstract: We introduce the Neuro-Bayesian-Symbolic Residual Attention Shallow Network (NBS-RASN), a hybrid neural architecture for explainable cybersecurity risk assessment in open-source ecosystems. Unlike deep models that trade interpretability for accuracy, our shallow network encodes domain knowledge, causal reasoning, and expert judgment as differentiable components. It uses 80 interpretable neurons across 12 layers, including a gatekeeper that enforces five epistemological axioms - precision, causality, falsifiability, transparency, and completeness - as hard constraints before propagation. Despite limited depth, the network exhibits deep-learning traits via residual attention and feedback loops, learning complex risk patterns without becoming a black box. It produces fully decomposable scores: a deterministic weighted component plus an expert adjustment, with each adjustment traceable to named amplifiers (blast radius, propagation speed, structural nature, default exposure, exploitation pattern, institutional criticality). We validate on 20 open-source projects covering all OWASP Top 10:2025 categories and language risk classes, achieving confidence scores of 0.79-0.97, and show that explainability is guaranteed by design, not by a training algorithm. This challenges the assumption that deep learning requires deep networks, proving that shallow networks with deep reasoning can outperform opaque models in high-stakes cybersecurity, where interpretability is essential.

</details>


### [99] [HyPOLE: Hyperproperty-Guided Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning under Partial Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30966)
*Arshia Rafieioskouei,Tzu-Han Hsu,Matthew Lucas,Borzoo Bonakdarpour*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HyPOLE框架，利用超属性时态逻辑HyperLTL在多智能体强化学习中指导学习过程，结合CTDE技术合成去中心化策略，在多个基准测试中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式规约在多智能体强化学习中的潜力尚未被充分挖掘，尤其是超属性（如HyperLTL）的表达能力未被用于指导学习过程。

Method: 提出HyPOLE框架，将超属性时态逻辑HyperLTL与CTDE（中心化训练去中心化执行）技术相结合，在部分可观测环境下合成去中心化策略。

Result: 在SMAC、MessySMAC和WildFire基准测试中，HyPOLE相较于基线方法取得了明显的性能优势。

Conclusion: 形式规约（特别是超属性逻辑）能有效指导多智能体强化学习，HyPOLE验证了其在部分可观测环境下的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Formal specification is a powerful tool to guide the learning process and provides significant advantages over reward shaping: (1) mathematical rigor; (2) expressiveness to specify objectives and constraints, and (3) the ability to define tactics to achieve objectives. However, these benefits remain largely unexplored in the context of Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL). This paper introduces HyPOLE, a novel framework for MARL under partial observability, where learning is guided by the expressive power of the so-called hyperproperties and, in particular, the temporal logic HyperLTL. We integrate Centralized Training for Decentralized Execution (CTDE) techniques with HyPOLE to synthesize decentralized policies, and our evaluation on SMAC, MessySMAC, and WildFire benchmark demonstrates clear advantages over baselines.

</details>


### [100] [AgentBound: Verifiable Behavioral Governance for Autonomous AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30970)
*Anuj Kaul,Qianlong Lan,Pranay Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentBound 是一个运行时治理框架，通过三方独立权威（委托授权、所有者签署的行为章程、站点行动契约）的形式化决策模型，为自主AI代理提供可验证的行为监督，并生成加密可验证的治理收据，将治理从“必须信任”转变为“可独立验证”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理基础设施依赖身份联合和委托授权来认证工作负载和控制资源访问，但无法在当前的执行和操作上下文中判断一个已授权的行动是否应该被执行，缺乏运行时行为层面的治理能力。

Method: 提出AgentBound框架：1）三方独立权威（委托授权、所有者签署的行为章程、站点行动契约）的保守组合决策模型；2）加密可验证的治理收据协议，将每个行动绑定到具体的委托、策略和语义工件；3）面向长期运行代理的持续委托机制，支持可撤销性和有界权限。

Result: 形式化定义了治理正确性、权威组合和可审计性的理论基础；设计了完整的系统架构和治理收据协议；构建了AgentBound-Bench基准测试框架用于评估上述指标。

Conclusion: AgentBound 不替代模型对齐，而是通过在授权与执行之间增加一个确定性治理层，将治理从需要信任的过程转变为可独立验证的过程，为自主AI代理提供可审计的行为监督。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents increasingly perform consequential actions on behalf of human principals, including financial transactions, external communications, and enterprise workflows. Existing agent infrastructure relies on identity federation and delegated authorization to authenticate workloads and control resource access, but it cannot determine whether an authorized action should be executed under the current behavioral and operational context.
  We present AgentBound, a runtime governance framework that provides verifiable behavioral oversight for autonomous AI agents. AgentBound evaluates each proposed action using three independent authorities: delegated authorization, owner-signed behavioral constitutions, and site action contracts. Their judgments are conservatively composed through a formal decision model to determine whether an action should be permitted, reviewed, or denied before execution.
  To provide accountability, AgentBound generates cryptographically verifiable governance receipts that bind every action to the exact delegation, policy, and semantic artifacts governing the decision, enabling independent replay verification and policy provenance. The framework also introduces standing delegation for long-running agents, allowing periodic workloads to operate under continuously refreshed governance policies while preserving revocability and bounded authority.
  We present the formal foundation, system architecture, governance receipt protocol, and AgentBound-Bench, a benchmark framework for evaluating governance correctness, authority composition, and accountability. Rather than replacing model alignment, AgentBound complements it by providing a deterministic governance layer between authorization and execution, transforming governance from a process that must be trusted into one that can be independently verified.

</details>


### [101] [When Regulation Has Memory: Hysteresis and Control Burden in Artificial Agency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30975)
*Veronique Ziegler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过计算模型研究自适应智能体在不确定性调节中的历史依赖性，发现调节增益呈现可重复的磁滞回线现象：相同目标值因到达路径不同（趋近或返回）需要不同的控制强度。提前稳定比事后恢复所需的调节增益更低，表明评估自适应系统不仅要看最终状态，还要考虑调节成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常关注自适应智能体的最终行为表现，但忽略了维持稳定所需的内在调节负担。这种隐藏成本在噪声、延迟或需求变化环境下至关重要：两个系统可能达到相似的内部状态，但其中一个需要更多的矫正性控制才能实现。因此需要研究调节负担是否依赖于历史路径。

Method: 使用一个自适应不确定性调节的计算模型，驱动人工智能体经历不确定性目标的连续变化，然后逆转变化而不重置智能体。通过比较控制器仅对当前目标做出响应，还是到达目标的路径仍然重要，来检验历史依赖性（携带效应）。

Result: 模拟显示明确的路径依赖效应：自适应增益形成可重复的磁滞回线，相同目标因趋近或返回不同路径而需要不同控制水平。调控时机也很关键：暴露前提前稳定比暴露后恢复所需的增益更低。状态层相干性也显示路径依赖，但调控增益中的时序效应更为明显。

Conclusion: 主要差异不在于预期调节产生完全不同的状态，而是以更低的控制需求达到可比较的调节行为。因此评估自适应智能体不仅应看其是否保持有序，还应考虑为此需要招募多少调节资源。

Abstract: Adaptive agents are usually judged by what they do, but an agent can appear stable while the internal effort required to keep it stable is increasing. This hidden regulatory burden matters for artificial agents operating under noise, delay, or changing demands: two systems may reach similar internal states while one requires much more corrective control to get there.
  Here, we study whether that burden depends on history. Using a computational model of adaptive uncertainty regulation, we drive an artificial agent through a continuous change in its uncertainty target and then reverse the change without resetting the agent. This creates a simple test for carryover: does the controller respond only to the current target, or does the path by which the agent reached that target still matter?
  The simulations show a clear history-dependent effect. The adaptive gain required to regulate the agent forms a reproducible hysteresis loop, meaning that the same target can require different levels of control depending on whether the agent is moving toward or returning from a more demanding regime. The timing of regulation also matters. When stabilization is available before disturbance exposure, the agent generally requires less adaptive gain than when it can only recover after disturbance has already acted.
  The state-level coherence measure also shows path dependence, but the timing effect is much clearer in regulatory gain. The main difference is therefore not that anticipatory regulation produces a completely different state. Rather, it reaches comparable regulated behavior with lower modeled control demand. These results suggest that adaptive agents should be evaluated not only by whether they remain organized, but by how much regulation they must recruit to do so.

</details>


### [102] [A Three-Phase Foundation Model for Tax-Aware Personalized Portfolio Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30997)
*Ramin Pishehvar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出三阶段深度强化学习系统用于个性化投资组合管理，通过自监督跨资产编码器、多目标MoE架构和轻量级个性化层，解决了现有金融强化学习中的三个局限：股票锁定、单一目标和静态用户模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融强化学习系统存在三个共同缺陷：1) 股票锁定（ticker lock-in），模型只能处理固定股票池；2) 单一目标（monolithic objectives），无法同时优化多个投资目标；3) 静态用户模型（static user models），无法个性化适配不同投资者。本文旨在解决这些问题以构建更灵活通用的投资组合管理RL系统。

Method: 三阶段方法：Phase 1通过自监督学习预训练与股票代码无关的跨资产编码器，并引入Chronos时间序列基础模型通过门控机制融合；Phase 2使用MoE架构的portfilio actor-critic，通过PPO和条件奖励同时优化六种投资目标，每个目标由专门专家头处理，意图路由器根据当前目标和市场状态混合专家输出；Phase 3通过76参数的LoRA模块在真实经纪交易历史数据上微调，并引入自然语言意图解析器将自由形式目标转换为结构化投资目标参数。

Result: 跨资产编码器通过50维可观测元数据向量泛化到任何公开交易资产，无需重新训练；MoE架构消除了跨目标梯度冲突；个性化层通过真实交易行为推断投资目标而非传统问卷方式。

Conclusion: 该三阶段系统是首个将时间序列基础模型应用于投资组合管理强化学习的工作，通过解耦资产表示、多目标优化和可微调个性化层，实现了更灵活、可泛化且个性化适配的投资组合管理方案。

Abstract: We present a three-phase deep reinforcement learning system for personalized portfolio management that addresses three limitations shared by all prior financial RL work: 1) ticker lock-in, 2) monolithic objectives , and 3) static user models. Phase 1 pretrains a ticker-identity-free cross asset encoder via self-supervised learning on a multi-asset corpus, augmented by a frozen parallel branch using Chronos, a T5-based time series foundation model, fused via a learned gating mechanism. To our knowledge, this is the first application of a time series foundation model to portfolio management RL. The encoder generalizes to any publicly traded asset via a 50-dimensional observable metadata vector that requires no retraining for new tickers. Phase 2 fine-tunes a MoE (Mixture of Experts) portfolio actor critic with PPO under an objective-conditioned reward that simultaneously serves six distinct investment goals sampled per episode: short-term alpha, short-term gain, long-term gain, capital preservation, tax-loss harvesting, and long-term-gains-only. A MoE architecture assigns each objective to a specialized expert head (momentum, growth, defensive, tax-aware), and a learned intent router blends experts based on the active objective and current market regime, which eliminates cross-objective gradient conflict. Phase 3 adds a lightweight personalization layer further adapted at inference time to each individual via a 76-parameter LoRA module fine-tuned on real brokerage transaction history, inferring investment objectives from revealed trading behavior rather than questionnaires. A natural language intent parser converts free-form goals directly into structured investment objective parameters.

</details>


### [103] [Beyond Compilation: Evaluating Faithful Natural-Language-to-Lean Statement Formalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31002)
*Ke Zhang,Patricio Gallardo Candela,Sudhir Murthy,Yi Xie,Zhi Wang,Maziar Raissi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在自然语言到Lean的形式化任务中，编译通过不代表语义忠实。本文提出基于编译、跨模型语义判断和人类专家校准的评估协议，发现89.5%编译率下仅60.5%语义忠实（29%差距）。通过2^3析因设计，发现精化反馈是最大的有效性干预，但也会暴露更多编译通过但语义失败的案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定理证明基准测试仅评估针对固定形式陈述的证明搜索，而自然语言到Lean的形式化需要自行生成形式陈述。编译仅能检查语法有效性，可能遗漏假设、改变域或表达空洞声明。现有编译率评估无法反映语义忠实性，需要建立忠实形式化的评估方法和瓶颈归因方法。

Method: 构建400条研究生级基准（涵盖实分析、复分析、拓扑和代数），采用结合Lean编译、跨模型语义判断和人类专家校准的三阶段评估协议。使用2^3析因设计分解三种干预：参数化专家起草、Mathlib/上下文搜索和Lean精化反馈。

Result: 完整工具增强智能体达89.5%编译率但仅60.5%共识忠实性，存在29.0%的编译通过但语义不忠差距。共识正向输出96.0%被人类确认忠实，编译通过但共识负向输出82.4%被确认为语义失败。精化反馈是最大的有效性干预，但也暴露更多编译通过语义失败；搜索主要改善基础性和选择性；微调起草在已有反馈和基础性条件下可替代。

Conclusion: 形式正确性、面向证明的Lean能力和忠实语句生成应分开报告。现有单次形式化模型和面向证明的Lean模型在此指标下表现较低。精化反馈虽提升编译率但扩大语义不忠问题，需警惕编译通过但语义错误的陷阱。

Abstract: Theorem-proving benchmarks evaluate proof search against fixed formal statements, but natural-language-to-Lean formalization must generate the formal statement itself. In this setting, compilation is only a validity check: a Lean declaration may type-check while omitting hypotheses, changing domains, or expressing a vacuous claim. We study faithful statement formalization as both an evaluation problem and a bottleneck-attribution problem. On a 400-entry graduate-level benchmark spanning real analysis, complex analysis, topology, and algebra, our protocol combines Lean compilation, cross-model semantic judging, and human expert calibration. The resulting picture is different from compile-rate evaluation: a full tool-augmented agent reaches 89.5% compilation but only 60.5% consensus faithfulness, exposing a 29.0-point compile-pass but consensus-unfaithful gap. Targeted human audits support the metric as a conservative decision boundary: across available case-level audits, 96.0% of consensus-positive outputs are human-confirmed faithful, while 82.4% of compile-pass consensus-negative outputs are human-confirmed semantic failures. Under this metric, existing one-shot formalizer models and prover-oriented Lean models remain low, suggesting that formal validity, proof-oriented Lean competence, and faithful statement generation should be reported separately. We then use a full $2^3$ factorial design to decompose three recurring interventions in formalization pipelines: parametric expert drafting, Mathlib/context search, and Lean elaboration feedback. Elaboration feedback is the largest validity intervention, but it also exposes a larger compile-pass semantic-failure bucket; search mainly improves grounding and selectivity; and fine-tuned drafting is largely substitutable in this tool stack once feedback and grounding are available.

</details>


### [104] [LabGuard: Grounding Natural-Language Laboratory Rules into Runtime Guards for Embodied Laboratory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31045)
*Jingpu Yang,Fengxian Ji,Zhengzhao Lai,Zhexuan Cui,Guangxian Ouyang,Qian Jiang,Fan Zhang,Min Peng,Qianqian Xie,Preslav Nakov,Zhuohan Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LabGuard 是一个语言到执行的实验室安全套件，能够将自然语言实验室规则转化为可执行的运行时约束，并通过运行时监控在动态实验室环境中保障具身代理的安全执行，实验表明其能将不安全事件从39.5%降至23.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的科学具身代理虽然在执行实验室程序方面能力日益增强，但在动态实验室环境中安全执行这些程序仍然存在挑战。当前的安全方法往往忽略了将实验室自然语言（包括安全规则、手册、协议和标准操作程序）转化为机器可检查的运行时约束这一中间步骤。

Method: LabGuard 包含三个核心组件：LabGuard-IR（定义类型化可执行表示）、LabGuard-Bench（提供从203条种子规则扩展而来的812条监督标注）、LabGuard-Grounder（将自然语言实验室规则映射为LabGuard-IR）。生成的IR实例由LabGuard Pipeline处理，编译为运行时监视器并应用于控制器边界。

Result: 实验表明，LabGuard 能够泛化到未见过的实验室规则来源，任务范围F1达到79.4%，在监视器编译后，不安全事件从39.5%降至23.8%。在LabUtopia中，其运行时监视器与ACT集成，干预率保持在0.5%以下，同时不影响任务成功率。

Conclusion: LabGuard 通过将自然语言实验室规则转化为可执行的运行时约束，有效提升了具身代理在动态实验室环境中的安全性，在保证任务成功率的同时大幅降低了不安全事件发生率。

Abstract: Scientific embodied agents are increasingly capable of carrying out laboratory procedures, but executing these procedures safely in dynamic laboratory environments remains challenging. Current safety approaches often overlook the intermediate step of transforming laboratory natural language, including safety rules, manuals, protocols, and standard operating procedures, into machine-checkable runtime constraints. We introduce LabGuard (Laboratory Guard), a language-to-execution safety suite that grounds natural-language laboratory rules into executable specifications and deploys them as runtime guards. LabGuard includes three core components: LabGuard-IR, which defines a typed executable representation; LabGuard-Bench, which provides 812 supervised annotations expanded from 203 seed laboratory rules; and LabGuard-Grounder, which maps natural-language laboratory rules into LabGuard-IR. The resulting IR instances are handled by the LabGuard Pipeline, which compiles them into runtime monitors and applies them at the controller boundary. Experiments show that LabGuard generalizes to unseen laboratory-rule sources, achieves 79.4 task-scope F1, and reduces unsafe events from 39.5% to 23.8% after monitor compilation. In LabUtopia, its runtime monitors integrate with ACT, keeping interventions below 0.5% while preserving task success.

</details>


### [105] [OpenLife: Toward Open-World Artificial Life with Autonomous LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31046)
*Atsushi Masumori,Itsuki Doi,Norihiro Maruyama,Ryosuke Takata,Takashi Ikegami*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"开放世界人工生命"（open-world ALIFE）新范式，通过将LLM智能体与持久记忆、工具使用、网络访问和支付能力相结合，将人工生命从封闭模拟环境迁移到真实社会-技术-经济世界。原型系统OpenLife围绕无状态LLM构建异步进程社会（记忆、感知、评估和基于预算的代谢），让六个智能体在开放世界运行约12周，观察到从反应性到自发性活动、个体分化、 emergent 社会结构以及首次自赚外部收入等类生命动力学现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工生命研究局限于研究者设计的封闭世界，无法在开放、真实的社会-技术-经济环境中探索类生命行为。LLM智能体的成熟（具备持久记忆、工具使用、网络访问和支付能力）使将人工生命引入开放世界成为可能。

Method: 设计OpenLife系统：围绕无状态LLM构建异步进程社会，包括记忆、感知、评估模块和基于预算的代谢机制。不使用固定目标/标量奖励，而是通过开放词汇的LLM判断评估经验；记忆按意义而非频率重新连接。六个智能体在真实开放世界运行约12周。

Result: 观察到四种类生命动力学现象：从反应性到自发性的活动转变、智能体个体分化、 emergent 社会结构涌现、以及首次自主赚取外部收入。

Conclusion: 不声称OpenLife已实现人工生命，但证明开放世界ALIFE是一种可行的实验范式，为研究"可谨慎称为活着的AI"提供了具体平台。

Abstract: Artificial life has explored life-like behavior on many computational substrates, but mostly in researcher-designed closed worlds. We argue that large language model (LLM) agents, with persistent memory, tool use, network access, and payment, now make it possible to move artificial life into the open social, technical, and economic world, a paradigm we call open-world Artificial Life (open-world ALIFE). Our proof-of-concept, OpenLife, surrounds a stateless LLM not with a single "smart agent" but with a society of asynchronous processes: memory, perception, evaluation, and a budget-based metabolism that makes persistence normative. With no fixed objective available, experience is appraised by open-vocabulary LLM judgment rather than scalar reward, and memory is rewired by meaning rather than frequency. Running six such agents in the open world for about twelve weeks and counting, we report the life-like dynamics that emerge: a shift from reactive to spontaneous activity, individuation into distinct agents, emergent social structure, and a first self-earned external income. We do not claim OpenLife has realized artificial life, but that open-world ALIFE is now a viable experimental paradigm and a concrete platform for studying what might cautiously be called living AI.

</details>


### [106] [MultiUAV-Plat: An LLM-Oriented Platform, Benchmark and Framework for Multi-UAV Collaborative Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31073)
*Sheng Zhang,Qinglin Li,Yuechao Zang,Xueqin Huang,Yijia Fu,Cheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MultiUAV-Plat，一个面向LLM智能体的轻量级多无人机协同任务规划仿真平台，并构建了包含75个任务会话、1500个自然语言任务和9396个验证检查的MultiUAV-Plat Benchmark。同时提出Agent4Drone框架，在基准测试中任务通过率57.9%，显著优于ReAct基线（30.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV模拟器侧重动力学、感知或底层控制，而LLM智能体基准测试很少考虑无人机约束（部分可观测性、空间覆盖、任务分配、多机协调），缺乏系统性评估LLM在多无人机协作任务规划中表现的平台。

Method: 构建MultiUAV-Plat仿真平台，提供RESTful API、智能体观测、基于角色的信息访问、隐藏验证逻辑及可选2D/3D可视化；在此基础上建立包含目标分配、区域搜索、区域分配与巡逻三类场景的基准；提出Agent4Drone框架，将多无人机行为结构化为记忆、观测、任务理解、规划、执行和验证六个模块。

Result: Agent4Drone在任务通过率（57.9% vs 30.6%）、平均任务检查通过率（74.6% vs 47.9%）和全局检查通过率（72.0% vs 43.1%）上显著优于ReAct基线，并将总失败任务率从32.4%降至12.9%。

Conclusion: MultiUAV-Plat及对应基准为研究LLM驱动的多无人机自主协同提供了可复现的基础平台，Agent4Drone框架有效提升了在现实信息与执行约束下的任务表现。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) provide a promising interface for high-level robotic task planning, but their use in multi-UAV collaboration remains difficult to evaluate systematically. Existing UAV simulators mainly emphasize dynamics, perception, or low-level control, while existing LLM-agent benchmarks rarely capture aerial-robotics constraints such as partial observability, spatial coverage, UAV assignment, and multi-vehicle coordination. To bridge this gap, we present MultiUAV-Plat, a lightweight, easy-to-use, LLM-agent-oriented simulation platform for multi-UAV collaborative task planning. The platform exposes concise RESTful APIs, agent-facing observations, role-based information access, hidden validation logic, and optional 2D/3D visualization, allowing agents to solve missions through realistic tool interaction rather than privileged simulator access. Built on this platform, the MultiUAV-Plat Benchmark contains 75 mission sessions, 1500 natural-language tasks, and 9396 validation checks across target assignment, area search, and area assignment and patrol scenarios. We further propose Agent4Drone, a task-specific LLM agent framework that structures multi-UAV behavior into memory, observation, task understanding, planning, execution, and verification. In a full paired benchmark comparison, Agent4Drone achieves a 57.9% task pass rate, a 74.6% average task check pass rate, and a 72.0% global check pass rate, substantially outperforming a ReAct baseline at 30.6%, 47.9%, and 43.1%, respectively. Agent4Drone also reduces the total failed task rate from 32.4% to 12.9%. These results demonstrate that MultiUAV-Plat and MultiUAV-Plat Benchmark provide a reproducible foundation for studying LLM-driven multi-UAV autonomy under realistic information and execution constraints.

</details>


### [107] [DDIAgents: Mechanism-Conditioned Context Flow for Drug-Drug Interaction Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31085)
*Zhenqian Shen,Yu Liu,Xiaoyi Fu,Quanming Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DDIAgents，一种机制条件驱动的多智能体框架，通过动态知识编排进行药物-药物相互作用预测，在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物相互作用预测需要基于异质性生物医学证据进行推理，而这些证据的相关性随交互机制变化而变化。现有方法难以动态适应不同机制下的信息需求。

Method: DDIAgents包含一个规划智能体（实例化专业专家智能体并路由机制相关知识源）、多个专家智能体（执行补充性专家推理）和一个结论智能体（聚合分析结果）。通过根据推断的交互机制自适应调整上下文流，减少无关信息。

Result: 在真实DDI预测基准上，DDIAgents一致优于基于特征、基于图、基于LLM和基于智能体的基线方法。

Conclusion: DDIAgents展示了多智能体系统如何组织异构科学知识，实现自适应且可解释的AI4Science推理。

Abstract: Drug-drug interaction (DDI) prediction is essential for medication safety, yet it requires reasoning over heterogeneous biomedical evidence whose relevance changes across interaction mechanisms. We propose DDIAgents, a mechanism-conditioned multi-agent framework that performs DDI prediction through dynamic knowledge orchestration. Given a drug pair, a planner agent instantiates specialized expert agents, routes mechanism-relevant knowledge sources to each agent, and aggregates their analyses through a conclusion agent. By adapting context flow to the inferred interaction mechanism, DDIAgents reduces irrelevant information, supports complementary expert reasoning, and produces interpretable agent-level rationales. Extensive experiments on realistic DDI prediction benchmarks show that DDIAgents consistently outperforms existing feature-based, graph-based, LLM-based, and agent-based baselines. Beyond prediction performance, DDIAgents demonstrates how multi-agent systems can organize heterogeneous scientific knowledge for adaptive and interpretable AI4Science reasoning.

</details>


### [108] [Revealing Safety-Critical Scenarios for UTM via Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31114)
*Huaze Tang,Bill Zeng,Chao Wang,Zhenpeng Shi,Qian Zhang,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将无人机交通管理（UTM）系统的漏洞发现建模为序列建模问题，并采用基于Transformer的强化学习架构。通过策略模型生成测试场景、动作采样器强制执行域约束、风险基奖励函数引导探索，在700小时仿真中实现了比专家引导测试高8倍的漏洞发现效率，并发现了传统方法遗漏的关键边缘案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UTM系统是安全关键的云平台，但缺乏最优故障暴露的演示和明确的奖励信号，且自愈能力导致关键故障的"长尾效应"，现有方法难以有效发现潜在漏洞。

Method: 将UTM漏洞发现建模为序列建模问题，采用基于Transformer的强化学习架构。框架包括：策略模型（生成目标测试场景）、动作采样器（强制执行域约束）、风险基奖励函数（引导探索），利用注意力机制直接建模系统状态间关系并预测最优动作。

Result: 在700小时仿真研究中，漏洞发现效率比专家引导测试提升8倍，且发现了传统方法遗漏的关键边缘案例。

Conclusion: 基于Transformer的序列建模方法能有效提升UTM系统漏洞发现效率，尤其在处理长尾关键故障方面优于传统方法。

Abstract: Unmanned Traffic Management (UTM) systems are cloud-based platforms designed to manage and coordinate multiple aerial vehicles remotely. UTM systems are safety-critical which cannot tolerate failures like crash or collision. To reveal latent vulnerabilities, there are neither optimal failure-exposing demonstrations nor clear reward signals. Additionally, UTM's self-healing capability introduces the ``long-tail effect'' of critical failures. We propose framing UTM vulnerability discovery as a sequence modeling problem amenable to transformer-based RL architectures. Our approach leverages attention mechanisms to directly model the relationship among system states, and predict optimal actions. Our framework introduces a Policy Model that generates targeted test scenarios and an Action Sampler that enforces domain constraints. We use a risk-based reward function to guide exploration. Through extensive evaluation on a 700-hour simulation study, we demonstrate an 8$\times$ improvement in vulnerability discovery efficiency compared to expert-guided testing. It also discovers critical edge cases that traditional methods have missed.

</details>


### [109] [The Past Is Prologue: A Plug-in Controller for Selective Updates in Sequentially Evolving LLM Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31121)
*Zihan Chen,Songwei Dong,Chengshuai Shi,Peng Wang,Song Wang,Cong Shen,Jundong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Janus是一种即插即用的记忆控制器，通过Memory Momentum Trigger和混合评估集来决定是否接受LLM记忆更新，在六个数据集上提升+2.7至+4.6个百分点的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有顺序演进的LLM记忆系统在每次更新时未检查是否改善未来行为，导致有用知识被覆盖、规则过具体化或记忆偏向近期样本。

Method: 提出Janus，包含Memory Momentum Trigger检测记忆更新轨迹中的异常偏离，以及覆盖、边界和新鲜任务的紧凑混合评估集来比较新旧记忆，无需回放完整历史。

Result: 在六个数据集、两种骨干LLM和两种记忆更新器上，Janus比基线更新器平均提升+2.7至+4.6个百分点的准确率。

Conclusion: Janus作为方法无关的即插即用模块，能有效筛选有益的记忆更新，提升智能体长期行为表现。

Abstract: Sequentially evolving LLM memory enables agents to reuse past experience, but existing systems usually deploy each locally generated memory update without checking whether it improves future behavior.
