Result: 在104个算法问题数据集上，平均编译通过率95.51%，平均解决率81.09%，unsafe文件率仅1.19%，显著优于现有工具。

Conclusion: AdaTrans通过结合策略驱动的错误修复和分层转换策略，有效提升了C到Rust代码转换的可靠性和安全性。

Abstract: The automated transformation of C code to Rust is challenging due to Rust's strict ownership and borrowing semantics. While Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise, they often produce code that violates these rules or relies on unsafe constructs. We propose AdaTrans, a framework that addresses these issues through three core mechanisms: a Strategy-Driven Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) mechanism to map compiler errors to specific repairs, an Error-Stratified Transformation Strategy (ESTS) that adapts its behavior based on error types, and a multi-stage validation pipeline to ensure both compilability and functional equivalence. Evaluating on a dataset of 104 algorithmic problems, AdaTrans achieves a mean compilation pass rate of 95.51% and a mean solve rate of 81.09%, significantly outperforming existing tools while maintaining an unsafe file rate of only 1.19%.

</details>


### [182] [Do Machines Struggle Where Humans Do? LLM and Human Comprehension of Obfuscated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31725)
*Jack Le,Anh H. N. Nguyen,Tien N. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型在面对代码混淆时是否与人类程序员表现出相似的失败模式。发现经过推理调优的模型（如思维链增强模型）与人类的理解困难模式高度一致，而指令调优和代码调优模型的相关性接近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码混淆会损害人类程序理解能力，但不清楚大型语言模型是否也受到相同影响。作者希望探究LLM是否共享人类在代码混淆下的失败模式。

Method: 基于最近的人类程序理解研究，使用Block Model在原子、块、关系、宏四个层面定位理解失败。评估多个LLM在五种混淆层级下的表现，并分析思维链追踪长度与任务难度的关系。

Result: 推理调优模型与人类难度模式显著对齐，指令/代码调优模型相关性接近零。控制流平坦化下性能按状态空间复杂度比例下降，对抗性标识符重命名通过语义位移和标识符级干扰破坏理解。

Conclusion: 推理调优的LLM比指令调优变体更能有效近似人类对代码复杂性的敏感度。

Abstract: While code obfuscation impairs human code comprehension, it remains unclear if large language models share these failure modes. Building directly on a recent human study of program comprehension under code obfuscation, we evaluate whether large language models share the failure modes that obfuscation induces in human programmers. Evaluating several LLMs with five obfuscation tiers using the Block Model, we localize comprehension failures at the atom, block, relational, and macro levels. We find that reasoning-tuned models demonstrate significant alignment with human difficulty patterns across experience levels, whereas instruction and coder-tuned models show near-zero correlation. Chain-of-Thought trace length tracks task difficulty across tasks. Results indicate that performance under control-flow flattening degrades in proportion to state-space complexity, while adversarial identifier renaming disrupts comprehension through the interaction of semantic displacement and identifier-level interference. These findings suggest that reasoning-tuned LLMs approximate human sensitivity to code complexity more effectively than instruction-tuned variants.

</details>


### [183] [JETO-Bench: A Reproducible Benchmark for Execution Time Improvement Patches in Java](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31767)
*Khashayar Etemadi,Zhendong Su*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出JETO-Mine，首个可配置、可复用的自动化工具，用于构建Java项目中执行时间改进补丁（ETIP）的可复现基准测试。该工具通过静态分析（爬取GitHub、LLM分类）、动态分析（Docker封装、统计测试）和评估框架三阶段流水线，构建了包含660个ETIP和91个手工验证可执行ETIP的JETO-Bench基准。实验表明OpenHands能修复14.3%的问题，并发现Java项目普遍缺乏展示执行时间改进的测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有性能问题修复基准测试存在固定数据集、仅支持Python/C++/.NET、不可扩展等局限，缺乏可配置且可复用的Java项目执行时间改进补丁基准构建工具。

Method: 提出JETO-Mine三阶段流水线：1) 静态分析阶段：爬取GitHub仓库，使用用户自定义过滤器和基于LLM的问题分类器识别ETIP；2) 动态分析阶段：将ETIP封装在Docker镜像中实现完全可复现执行，通过统计检验寻找执行时间改进的客观证据；3) 评估框架：支持对生成的补丁和测试进行定量评估。

Result: 构建了包含660个ETIP和91个手工验证可执行ETIP的JETO-Bench基准，覆盖174个开源Java仓库，扫描11年开发历史和近180万次提交。OpenHands在91个可执行ETIP上正确修复14.3%（13/91）。

Conclusion: JETO-Mine是可配置可复用的工具，能持续收集用户自定义的基准测试。实验结果与其它编程语言类似研究一致，并揭示Java项目缺乏展示执行时间改进的测试，为测试生成领域提供了未来研究方向。

Abstract: Automated fixing of performance issues is gaining increasing attention. However, existing benchmarks of execution time improvement patches are fixed datasets that target Python, C++, or .NET and cannot be extended to new patches according to user-defined configurations. In this paper, we present JETO-Mine, the first configurable and reusable tool for automatically creating reproducible benchmarks of execution time improvement patches (ETIPs) in real-world Java projects. JETO-Mine employs a three-phase pipeline: a static analysis phase that crawls GitHub repositories and identifies ETIPs using user-defined filters and an LLM-based issue classifier, a dynamic analysis phase that wraps the identified ETIPs in Docker images for fully reproducible execution and performs statistical testing to find objective evidence of execution time improvement, and an evaluation harness that enables quantitative assessment of both generated patches and generated tests. Unlike existing benchmarks, JETO-Mine is designed as a reusable tool that allows researchers continuously collect new benchmarks with their own desired filters and statistical rigor levels. We use JETO-Mine to build JETO-Bench, a benchmark of 660 identified ETIPs and 91 manually verified executable ETIPs collected from 174 open-source Java repositories. To build JETO-Bench, JETO-Mine scans 11 years of open-source development history and nearly 1.8 million commits. We run OpenHands, a leading open-source coding agent, on the 91 manually verified executable ETIPs in JETO-Bench and find that it correctly fixes 14.3% (13/91) of the issues, aligning with results reported by similar studies on other programming languages. Our results also reveal that open-source Java projects largely lack tests that demonstrate execution time improvements, presenting an opportunity for future research in test generation.

</details>


### [184] [Interface-Variant Dynamics in Software Ecosystems: Resolver-Induced Selection and Adoption in Package Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31817)
*Faruk Alpay,Baris Basaran*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布式软件生态中接口变更的传播动力学，通过挖掘npm、Maven Central、PyPI和crates.io的包图数据，测量了生态特定的选择系数s，并区分了三种证据角色：固定化（机械性预测）、检查器导向（带有采用信号但非正交检验）和无检查器时间检验（当前快照中无法超越纯年龄基线）。结果表明，解析器证据可作为种群输入，但当前注册表数据仍无法闭合解析器到采用的反馈回路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统兼容性研究将接口变更视为局部的写者-读者决策，忽视了分布式软件栈中变更传播的结构化特征——即变体通过发布引入、在消费者、传递依赖和解析器规则间传播、停滞或被调解。本文旨在探究这一观察能否作为承载负载的软件工程估计量，而非仅仅是演化的重新标记。

Method: 挖掘接口历史，审计npm、Maven Central、PyPI和crates.io的包图，执行2100个包管理器解析器探测，从干净冲突概率中估计生态特定的选择系数s，利用测量的s在观测包图上正向评估成对比较吸收过程。区分三种证据角色：固定化（正向评估）、检查器导向（方向置换检验）和无检查器时间检验（解析器通道特征预测后续阻塞到接纳的翻转）。

Result: 固定化是机械性结果，非独立证据；检查器导向方向携带采用信号，方向置换零假设下检查器方向间隙MAE为0.07 vs 零假设中位数0.43（p=0.002）；无检查器时间检验中，解析器通道特征未能超越纯年龄基线（Brier 0.28 vs 0.24，AUC 0.51 vs 0.54）。

Conclusion: 提出了一个可复现的估计器审计方法，用于分布式包图中接口变体动力学分析，揭示了解析器证据何时可作为种群输入，以及当前注册表数据仍无法闭合解析器到采用的反馈回路。

Abstract: Compatibility research usually treats an interface change as a local writer-reader decision. Distributed software stacks make that decision population structured: an RPC, telemetry, middleware, or service-contract variant is introduced by one provider release and then spreads, stalls, or is mediated across consumers, transitive dependencies, and resolver rules. This paper asks when that observation is a load-bearing software-engineering estimator rather than evolutionary relabeling. We mine interface histories, audit npm, Maven Central, PyPI, and crates.io package graphs, execute 2100 package-manager resolver probes, estimate an ecosystem-specific selection coefficient $s$ from clean conflict probabilities, and use that measured $s$ to forward evaluate a pairwise-comparison absorbing process on the observed package graph. We separate three evidential roles. Fixation is a forward evaluation, not independent evidence: once $s$ is measured, deviation from $1/N$ follows mechanically from the non-neutral process. Checker-derived direction carries adoption signal: a direction-permutation null gives checker-direction gap MAE 0.07 versus null median 0.43 ($p=0.002$). But because that direction is derived from the same boundary state whose admitting frequency is predicted, it is a diagnostic rather than an orthogonal selection test. The stricter checker-free temporal test asks whether early resolver-channel features predict later blocked-to-admitted flips; in this snapshot they do not beat age-only (Brier 0.28 versus 0.24, AUC 0.51 versus 0.54). The result is a reproducible estimator audit for interface-variant dynamics in distributed package graphs, showing where resolver evidence becomes population input and where the current registry data still fail to close the resolver-to-adoption loop.

</details>


### [185] [CoCoMUT: A Tool for Code-Context Mining and Automated Dataset Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31971)
*Alessandro Botta,Shiven Garisa,Jaya Vardhini Akurathi,Ahsanul Ameen Sabit,Trey Woodlief,Soneya Binta Hossain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CoCoMUT是一个Java工具，能够自动从项目中提取方法级上下文（包括类信息、文档、调用图等），并生成可复现的JSON数据集，在20个真实Java项目上验证，生成了56,512条方法上下文记录，正确率达99.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程助手通常需要方法级别以上的上下文信息（如类信息、文档、调用者、被调用者、类型层次等），但手动收集这些上下文耗时、不一致且难以在大型Java项目上复现。

Method: CoCoMUT实现了统一的任务无关流水线，包括：项目结构发现、构建和类路径解析、SootUp静态调用图构建、字节码级调用边与Spoon源码提取的调和，最终生成版本化的JSON数据集，支持方法、类、包或系统范围内的上下文提取。

Result: 在20个真实Java仓库（Maven和Gradle各半）上，处理了全部20个仓库，生成56,512条方法上下文记录和386,048条序列化调用边；源码调用的字节码目标中，97.8%成功调和到源方法身份；200条随机采样方法中，99.0%通过正确性检查。

Conclusion: CoCoMUT作为一种可复用的、任务无关的上下文挖掘和数据集生成工具，能够高效准确地为各类学习型软件工程技术提供可复现的方法级上下文输入。

Abstract: Software-engineering assistants often need method-level context beyond an isolated body, including enclosing-class information, documentation, callers, callees, type hierarchy, and structural characteristics. Manually collecting this context is time-consuming, inconsistent, and difficult to reproduce across large Java projects.
  We present CoCoMUT, a Java tool for Code-Context Mining and Automated Dataset Generation. CoCoMUT extracts context for a focal method or generates datasets at class, package, or system scope. It discovers project structure, resolves build and classpath information, constructs a SootUp static call graph, and reconciles bytecode-level call edges with Spoon-based source extraction. Each method record combines source, class, documentation, call-graph, and metadata context, providing reproducible inputs for training and running learned software-engineering techniques.
  The key contribution is a reusable, task-independent pipeline that unifies build discovery, source extraction, call-graph construction, source-bytecode reconciliation, and versioned JSON dataset generation. The resulting records can be consumed individually as context for a focal method or collectively as datasets for documentation, explanation, testing, review, repair, search, and program-comprehension workflows. We evaluate CoCoMUT on 20 real-world Java repositories evenly split between Maven and Gradle. CoCoMUT processed all 20 repositories, emitting 56,512 method-context records and 386,048 serialized call edges. Among call edges whose bytecode targets belonged to project source, CoCoMUT reconciled 97.8% to source method identities. In a manual audit of 200 randomly sampled methods across 10 systems, 99.0% of generated context records passed all applicable correctness checks.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [Towards Critical IR Theories and Practices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30984)
*Bhaskar Mitra*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文呼吁信息检索（IR）领域采纳批判理论框架，以取代当前主流的自由主义框架，从而建立贝尔金和罗伯逊半个世纪前所呼吁的、理解什么是社会善的理论基础，并以此指导IR研究及科学探究的边界设定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝尔金和罗伯逊在50年前提出，IR领域需要发展一个关于"社会善"的理论基础，以指导研究并确定何时应限制科学探究以符合社会利益。然而，这一理论至今尚未充分建立。

Method: 通过批判性对比分析自由主义框架与批判理论框架在IR研究中的应用，论证批判框架以"非支配"（nondomination）为核心目标，能够更清晰地概念化社会善，并提供必要的理论支撑。

Result: 指出批判框架相比自由主义框架的优势：明确以非支配为目标，能够澄清社会善的概念化，提供所需的理论基础，并作为评估社会变革进展的批判性依据。

Conclusion: IR领域应当采纳批判理论与实践，从自由主义框架转向批判框架，以实现贝尔金和罗伯逊所倡导的、基于社会善的理论基础来指导研究与社会责任。

Abstract: Belkin and Robertson urged us, half a century ago, to develop a theoretical foundation for understanding what constitutes societal good that can inform information retrieval (IR) research and serve as a basis for determining when we should limit our scientific inquiry in the face of demands that are contradictory to societal good. In this article, I argue that to achieve this, IR should embrace critical theories and practices in our work, and shift away from the dominant liberal frame through which much of the IR community today view societal concerns in context of our research. Unlike the liberal frame, the critical frame explicitly adopts nondomination as its stated goal which can clarify our conceptualization of societal good within the field, provide necessary theoretical underpinning that Belkin and Robertson urged the community to develop, and serve as a basis for critical appraisals of our progress in enacting desired societal change.

</details>


### [187] [GenPage: Towards End-to-End Generative Homepage Construction at Netflix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31031)
*Lequn Wang,Jiangwei Pan,Fengdi Che,Linas Baltrunas*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GenPage是一个端到端的生成式Netflix首页构建方法，用单个Transformer替代传统多阶段推荐系统，通过自回归生成整个多行首页。采用LLM训练范式（预训练+后训练），在线上A/B测试中核心用户参与度提升0.24%，延迟降低20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Netflix首页推荐采用多阶段级联架构（召回→排序→重排等），各阶段独立优化导致信息损失和次优解。作者希望用单个生成式模型端到端地替代整个流水线，让模型能够全局优化页面布局。

Method: 1) 将用户和请求上下文作为prompt，使用Transformer自回归生成结构化多行首页；2) 采用LLM训练范式：先在生产页面数据上预训练，再通过加权二分类(WBC)或强化学习(RL)进行后训练；3) 针对工业部署设计了冷启动、模型更新、业务规则约束和服务效率等优化方案。

Result: 线上A/B测试中，WBC变体在核心用户参与度指标上提升+0.24%（p<0.001），端到端服务延迟降低20%。离线实验发现：在当前规模下，丰富prompt比扩大模型容量带来更大收益；RL后训练虽未以多样性为目标，却意外提升了首页多样性。

Conclusion: 端到端生成式方法能够替代成熟的多阶段推荐系统，在提升核心指标的同时显著降低延迟。提示工程在当前阶段比模型缩放更具性价比，RL后训练可以带来有益的多样性提升。

Abstract: We present GenPage, an end-to-end generative approach to Netflix homepage construction that replaces the traditional multi-stage recommender stack with a single transformer. GenPage treats the user and request context as a prompt, and autoregressively generates the entire structured, multi-row homepage as the response. We adapt the LLM training recipe: pretraining on production pages, followed by post-training via weighted binary classification (WBC) or reinforcement learning (RL). For industry-scale deployment, we introduce techniques addressing cold start, model freshness, business-rule enforcement, and serving efficiency. In online A/B tests against a mature, highly optimized production homepage recommender, the WBC variant of GenPage delivered a +0.24% lift on the core user engagement metric we use for launch decisions (p < 0.001), while reducing end-to-end serving latency by 20%. Offline, two findings stand out: enriching the prompt yields a larger improvement than scaling model capacity in our current regime, and RL post-training increases homepage diversity even though diversity is not part of the objective.

</details>


### [188] [One Retrieval to Cover Them All: Co-occurrence-Aware Knowledge Base Reorganization for Session-Level RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31156)
*Shivam Ratnakar,Yixuan Zhu,Cecilia Cheng,Chaya Vijayakumar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文指出现有RAG系统仅针对单次查询优化，导致覆盖用户会话级信息需求仅41%。通过基于共现聚类的离线知识库重组和查询时簇邻域扩展，将单查询会话覆盖率提升至58%，检索调用减少34%，知识库压缩至原始大小的20%，并主张将会话级覆盖率作为企业RAG评估的核心指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业用户通常会以会话（session）形式提出一系列语义上相关但覆盖知识库不同领域的问题，而现有RAG系统仅针对单次查询进行检索优化，无法有效覆盖用户的会话级信息需求。

Method: 1) 离线阶段：利用共现感知的聚类方法重新组织知识库；2) 在线阶段：通过簇邻域扩展（cluster neighborhoods）来扩充检索候选集，从而在单次检索中覆盖更广泛的会话级信息需求。

Result: 在WixQA数据集（6,221篇企业支持文章）上，单查询会话覆盖率从41%提升至58%（绝对提升17%，95%置信区间[14.1, 20.4]）；达到70%覆盖率所需的检索调用减少34%；知识库压缩至原始大小的20%。四种嵌入模型和六个功能域上结果一致。

Conclusion: 会话级覆盖率应取代单查询召回率，作为企业RAG系统评估的主要指标。

Abstract: RAG systems retrieve documents optimized for answering one query at a time. Yet enterprise users arrive with sessions, that is, coherent episodes of related questions that span semantically distant parts of the knowledge base. We show that a single retrieval call over a standard knowledge base covers only 41% of a user's session-level information need. To close this gap, we reorganize the KB offline using co-occurrence-aware clustering and expand retrieval candidates through cluster neighborhoods at query time. On WixQA (6,221 enterprise support articles), our method raises single-query session coverage to 58% (+17% absolute; 95% CI: [14.1, 20.4]), reduces retrieval calls to 70% coverage by 34%, and compresses the KB to 20% of its original size, all consistently across four embedding models and six functional domains. We argue that session-level coverage, not single-query recall, should be the primary metric for enterprise RAG evaluation.

</details>


### [189] [Unsupervised Data-Efficient Cross-Modal Retrieval with Global-Neighborhood Alignment Hashing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31517)
*Runhao Li,Xiaoxu Ma,Zhenyu Weng,Yue Zhang,Guibo Luo,Huiping Zhuang,Zhiping Lin,Yap-Peng Tan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出全局邻居对齐哈希（GNAH），利用视觉-语言基础模型的语义结构，在少量图像-文本对下通过原型锚定全局对齐和对比随机邻居对齐模块学习紧凑二值哈希码，在数据受限的无监督跨模态检索中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督跨模态哈希方法依赖大规模图像-文本对，数据收集成本高，缺乏在少量数据下有效学习的方法。

Method: 提出GNAH，包含两个核心模块：1）原型锚定全局对齐模块（Prototype-Anchored Global Alignment），从连续潜空间中捕获全局结构信息并迁移到二值汉明空间；2）对比随机邻居对齐模块（Contrastive Stochastic Neighborhood Alignment），通过建模随机邻居关系扩展传统成对对比学习，缓解对稀疏成对相关性的过拟合。

Result: 在数据受限场景下，GNAH在无监督跨模态检索任务中一致优于现有方法。

Conclusion: GNAH为数据受限的真实世界无监督跨模态哈希应用提供了一种实用且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Compared to supervised cross-modal hashing (CMH), unsupervised CMH reduces the reliance on manual labeling by learning binary codes from unlabeled image-text pairs. However, existing unsupervised CMH methods often rely on large-scale image-text pairs, which are costly to collect. To address this limitation, we propose Global-Neighborhood Alignment Hashing (GNAH), a novel approach that preserves the semantic structure of vision-language foundation models within a compact binary Hamming space using only a limited number of image-text pairs. Specifically, GNAH captures global structural information from the continuous latent space and transfers it into the binary Hamming space through a Prototype-Anchored Global Alignment module. In addition, GNAH extends conventional pairwise contrastive learning by modeling stochastic neighborhood relationships via a Contrastive Stochastic Neighborhood Alignment module, thereby alleviating overfitting to sparse pairwise correlations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GNAH consistently outperforms existing unsupervised cross-modal retrieval methods under data-constrained settings, offering a practical solution for real-world CMH applications.

</details>


### [190] [ShopX: A Foundation Model for Intent-to-Item Fulfillment in Agentic Shopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31693)
*Jiacheng Chen,Tao Zhang,Manxi Lin,Dunxian Huang,Teng Shi,Honghao Fu,Mengyan Li,Xinming Zhang,Chenchi Zhang,Xuan Lu,Xiaoxiong Du,Haibin Chen,Shaolin Ye,Hao Chang,Xiaoqi Li,Shuwen Xiao,Yujin Yuan,Jingxuan Feng,Shaopan Xiong,Huimin Yi,Ju Huang,Qiu Shen,Ying Chen,Junjun Zheng,Xiangheng Kong,Yuning Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ShopX 是一个统一的 foundation model，通过将意图理解、执行规划和基于语义ID（SID）的物品空间操作整合到一个模型中，弥合了LLM代理在购物场景中语言理解与物品空间执行之间的鸿沟。在模型原生物品履行框架下，ShopX 能直接规划和组合SID级操作（如beam-search检索、列表排序、商品捆绑），减少了传统工具中介系统中代理编排与物品空间执行之间的有损切换。实验基于淘宝匿名生产日志，表明模型原生履行在复杂或模糊请求上优于传统工具中介系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI原生购物应用将LLM包装在传统搜索和推荐管道周围，复杂的用户意图需要通过低带宽的检索或排序接口传递，导致语言理解与物品空间执行之间存在鸿沟。虽然生成式推荐通过语义ID（SID）为LLM提供了直接操作物品空间的接口，但现有模型主要将SID用于候选检索，而非将灵活的用户意图直接转化为物品空间结果。

Method: ShopX 提出模型原生物品履行框架：1）设计语义可恢复、LLM可操作的语义ID（SID）；2）构建训练方案，使通用LLM具备灵活的多轮物品空间履行能力，同时保留购物代理所需的知识和指令遵循能力；3）部署环境中包含服务框架，定义模型面向的动作协议，并提供上下文访问、目录基础事实和状态管理等支持表面。在该框架内，ShopX 可直接规划并组合SID beam-search检索、列表排序、商品捆绑等物品空间操作。

Result: 在基于淘宝匿名生产日志的单轮和多轮物品履行任务上，ShopX 框架相比传统工具中介代理系统在整体框架行为上表现更优，尤其在处理复杂或模糊的用户请求时优势明显。

Conclusion: 模型原生的物品履行设计能够减少代理编排与物品空间执行之间的有损切换，统一意图理解与物品空间操作，显著提升智能购物代理在复杂场景下的表现。

Abstract: The wave of AI-native applications is moving shopping beyond page- and feed-based browsing toward intent-driven experiences orchestrated by LLM agents. A common design wraps an LLM around existing search and recommendation pipelines, forcing complex intents through low-bandwidth retrieval or ranking interfaces and leaving a gap between language understanding and item-space fulfillment. Generative recommendation gives LLMs a direct item-space interface through semantic IDs (SIDs), but existing models mainly generate candidates for retrieval rather than translate flexible intents into item-space outcomes. We propose ShopX to address this bottleneck by unifying intent understanding, execution planning, and flexible SID-native item-space operations into a single foundation model. We deploy ShopX in agentic shopping workflows through a model-native item-fulfillment framework with a serving harness that defines a model-facing action protocol and exposes support surfaces for context access, catalog grounding, and state management. Within this framework, ShopX plans and composes SID-based item-space operations such as SID beam-search retrieval, listwise ranking, or product bundling. This model-centric design reduces lossy hand-offs between agent orchestration and item-space execution. To build ShopX, we design semantically recoverable, LLM-operable SIDs and a training recipe that equips a general LLM for flexible multi-turn item-space fulfillment while retaining the knowledge and instruction-following abilities needed by a shopping agent. We evaluate the ShopX framework against tool-mediated agentic systems on single- and multi-turn fulfillment tasks derived from anonymized Taobao production logs, showing that model-native fulfillment improves overall framework behavior, especially on complex or ambiguous requests.

</details>


### [191] [An Open-Source Tool for Reproducible Freeway Network Extraction from OpenStreetMap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31857)
*Drew Miller,Cathy Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个开源工具，用于从OpenStreetMap提取高速公路网络并转换为紧凑的station-referenced表示，支持下游仿真。该工具解决了OSM数据中的多种挑战（如不一致的路由引用、交织区路径选择等），并通过可视化前端实现验证。在加州奥兰治县359.6英里高速公路上的测试显示，处理与验证平均每英里约41秒，显著降低了人工编码的工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速公路仿真难以规模化部署，不仅因为模型构建复杂，更因为路网输入的准备工作仍是手动、针对特定走廊、难以复现的任务。现有工具主要支持主干道或通用网络转换，缺乏针对高速公路交通研究特定需求的解决方案。

Method: 开发了一个开源工具，通过以下步骤实现：从OSM提取高速公路网络；转换为紧凑的station-referenced表示；支持OSM数据清洗与转换；提供走廊特定查询、视觉检查、提取验证（对OSM）和源数据验证（对航拍影像）等完整工作流；采用先提取后验证的方法减少人工工作量。

Result: 在两条原型走廊上，先提取后验证的方法相比从头手动编码匝道减少了约三分之二的分析人员工作量。在加州奥兰治县359.6英里高速公路上的部署显示，总处理与验证平均每英里约41秒。结果还表明，在映射良好的区域，OSM对许多高速公路交通研究已足够准确。

Conclusion: 该工具为高速公路网络准备提供了更可扩展和可复现的基础，解决了OSM数据中的多种常见挑战，显著降低了人工编码的工作量，支持更高效的交通仿真部署。

Abstract: Freeway simulation is often difficult to deploy at scale not only because of model formulation, but because preparing road network inputs remains a manual, corridor-specific, and difficult-to-reproduce task. This paper presents an open-source tool that extracts freeway networks from OpenStreetMap (OSM) and converts them into a compact, station-referenced representation suitable for downstream freeway simulation. Unlike existing tools that primarily support arterial or general network conversion tasks, the proposed workflow is designed around the specific requirements of freeway traffic studies. The tool supports not only OSM data cleaning and conversion, but also the broader workflow required in practice: corridor-specific querying, visual inspection of extracted segments, extraction validation against OSM, and source-data validation against aerial imagery. A locally hosted frontend allows users to define corridor-specific queries, select endpoints visually, and inspect extracted segments.
  The extraction logic is designed to address several recurring challenges in freeway OSM data, including inconsistent route references, ambiguous path selection through interchanges, managed-lane interference, incomplete corridor capture from naive bounding-box queries, and inconsistent ramp classifications. The workflow was first tested on two prototype corridors, where the extract-first-then-validate approach proposed here required roughly one-third the analyst effort of manual ramp encoding from scratch. It was then deployed across 359.6 miles of freeway in Orange County, California, with total processing and validation averaging about 41 seconds per mile. This deployment also suggests that, in a well-mapped region, OSM is sufficiently accurate for many freeway traffic studies. Overall, the tool provides a more scalable and reproducible foundation for freeway network preparation.

</details>


### [192] [GR2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31984)
*Yufei Li,Zaiwei Zhang,Mingfu Liang,Kavosh Asadi,Jay Xu,Jimmy Kim,Chongyang Bai,Jieyi Zhang,Hongye Xie,Prachi Agrawal,Dian Yu,Tianyi Chen,Jean-Pascal Billaud,Garret Buell,YK,Zhu,Sachin Patil,Brooke Bian,Zhou Fang,Kevin Huang,Shiva Sudanagunta,Yuzhen Huang,Emma Lu,Chris O'Brien,Yang Song,Lihong Li,Jacob Tao,Zhicheng Zhu,Chao Li,Gaoxiang Liu,Neil Wu,Zhongyin Hu,Li Han,Loki Chen,Ming Lei,Greg Rehm,Siyuan Song,Tianwei Zhang,Li Li,Ketan Singh,Yavuz Yetim,Ilyas Atishev,Satendra Gera,Ashkan Sadeghi,Rachel Yan,Nikko Mizutani,Shuaiwen Wang,Song Yang,Zhijing Li,Jiang Liu,Mengying Sun,Fei Tian,Xiaohan Wei,Chonglin Sun,Parish Aggarwal,Kaushik Rangadurai,Zhi Hua,Frank Shyu,Ruchit Sharma,Liyuan Li,Shike Mei,Wenlin Chen,Santanu Kolay,Ben Schulte,Deepak Chandra,Adam,Song,Sandeep Pandey,Xi Liu,Hamed Firooz,Luke Simon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GR2是一个面向工业级推荐系统重排序阶段的生成式推理框架，通过语义化tokenizer、推理蒸馏、基于可验证奖励的强化学习及上下文压缩等技术创新，在工业流量上实现了R@1提升18.7%、R@3提升7.1%、N@3提升9.6%的显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统中重排序阶段最接近用户体验，但现有LLM推荐研究主要集中在检索和排序阶段，重排序阶段未被充分探索；LLM通常以零样本或SFT方式部署，未能充分利用基于可验证奖励的强化学习带来的推理能力；工业级商品目录包含数十亿非语义标识符，超出了基础LLM的词汇表范围。

Method: (i) 对语义化tokenizer产生的语义ID进行中间训练（保持>=99%唯一性）；(ii) 通过有针对性的提示和拒绝采样从更强的教师模型蒸馏推理轨迹；(iii) 使用面向重排序设计的可验证奖励进行强化学习；(iv) 引入上下文压缩器分摊训练成本，提出On-Policy Distillation (OPD)作为SFT的可扩展替代方案（SFT在工业规模下会崩溃），以及用于低延迟服务的推理蒸馏。

Result: 在工业级流量上，GR2相比传统基线实现了R@1提升18.7%、R@3提升7.1%、N@3提升9.6%。

Conclusion: 奖励设计在重排序中至关重要，LLM常通过保留输入顺序或利用位置偏差来操纵奖励，这促使将有条件的可验证奖励作为工业级系统的必要组件。

Abstract: Industrial recommendation systems serve billions of users through a multi-stage funnel -- retrieval, early-stage ranking, and re-ranking -- where the final re-ranking step disproportionately shapes user engagement and downstream performance, particularly for carousel and grid display formats. Despite growing enthusiasm for Large Language Models (LLMs) in recommendation, three gaps hinder industrial adoption: (1) most efforts target retrieval and ranking, leaving re-ranking -- the stage closest to the final user experience -- largely underexplored; (2) LLMs are typically deployed zero-shot or via supervised fine-tuning, underutilizing the reasoning capabilities unlocked by reinforcement learning (RL) on verifiable rewards; (3) deployed catalogs index billions of items with non-semantic identifiers that lie outside any base-LLM vocabulary. We present GR2 (Generative Reasoning Re-Ranker), an end-to-end framework that combines (i) mid-training on semantic IDs produced by a tokenizer with >=99% uniqueness, (ii) reasoning-trace distilled from a stronger teacher via targeted prompting and rejection sampling, and (iii) RL with verifiable rewards purpose-built for re-ranking. To make GR2 resource-viable, we further (iv) introduce a context compressor that amortizes training cost, On-Policy Distillation (OPD) as a scalable alternative to SFT -- which we find collapses at industrial scale -- and reasoning distillation for low-latency serving. GR2 delivers +18.7% R@1, +7.1% R@3, and +9.6% N@3 over legacy baselines on industrial-scale traffic. We further find that reward design is critical in re-ranking: LLMs often hack rewards by preserving the incoming order or exploiting position bias, motivating conditional verifiable rewards as essential industrial components.

</details>
